    doLast {
        def configDir = file("src/main/resources/config")
        configDir.mkdirs()
        def configFile = file("src/main/resources/config/coreservermod.json")
        if (!configFile.exists()) {
            configFile.write('''{
  "sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled": true,
  "sugar_cane_growth_interval_seconds": 1,
  "sapling_growth_time_seconds": 15,
  "crop_growth_time_seconds": 15,
  "iron_golem_spawn_enabled": true,
  "iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds": 5,
  "max_automatic_golems_per_area": 10,
  "animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled": true,
  "baby_growth_time_seconds": 15,
  "instant_growth_when_fed": true,
  "sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds": 5,
  "villager_emerald_drop_enabled": true,
  "villager_emerald_drop_chance": 0.4,
  "guaranteed_chicken_from_egg": true,
  "chicken_egg_interval_seconds": 20
}''')
        }
    }
}

    dependsOn("generateConfig")

rootProject.name = "CoreServerMod"====
# CoreServerMod

A Fabric server-side mod for Minecraft Java Edition 1.21.4 that accelerates various game mechanics to reduce waiting times and automate repetitive tasks.

## Features

### 1. Sugar Cane on Soul Sand
- Allows sugar cane placement on soul sand
- Grows by one block approximately every second when planted on soul sand
- Preserves vanilla growth behavior on other valid substrates

### 2. Accelerated Tree Growth
- All vanilla saplings and propagules grow into their correct trees within approximately 15 seconds
- Requires valid environmental and space conditions

### 3. Iron Block Villager
- Villagers standing on iron blocks become special golem-spawning villagers
- Attempts to spawn exactly one iron golem every 100 server ticks (5 seconds) when a valid spawn position exists
- Does not require beds, village membership, gossip, panic, nearby mobs, professions, or other vanilla golem eligibility checks
- Moving off the iron block disables special behavior; standing on one again reactivates it with a fresh 5-second interval
- Other villagers retain vanilla behavior

### 4. Accelerated Crop Growth
- Applicable vanilla crops and plants grow to maturity within approximately 15 seconds
- Preserves necessary soil, light, water, and plant-specific requirements

### 5. Animal Breeding
- Removes breeding cooldown for supported breedable vanilla animals
- Allows repeated breeding whenever normal eligibility conditions are met
- Baby animals mature within approximately 15 seconds or instantly when fed once (configurable)

### 6. Sheep Wool Regrowth
- Sheep regrow wool and become shearable approximately every 5 seconds
- Preserves normal shearing drops and prevents duplicate drops

### 7. Villager Emerald Drops
- Villagers have a 40% chance to drop one emerald on successful trade
- Evaluates chance once per trade event and prevents duplicate rewards

### 8. Guaranteed Chicken from Eggs
- Every player-thrown chicken egg spawns exactly one chicken
- Bypasses vanilla random hatch chance without duplicate spawning

### 9. Chicken Egg Production
- Adult chickens produce one egg approximately every 20 seconds regardless of location or movement
- Baby chickens do not lay eggs

## Installation

1. Install Fabric Loader 0.16.9+ for Minecraft 1.21.4
2. Install Fabric API 0.118.0+1.21.4
3. Place `CoreServerMod-1.0.0.jar` in your server's `mods` folder
4. Start the server - a default configuration file will be generated at `config/coreservermod.json`

## Configuration

The mod generates a configuration file at `config/coreservermod.json` on first launch. All features can be toggled and intervals adjusted:

```json
{
  "sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled": true,
  "sugar_cane_growth_interval_seconds": 1,
  "sapling_growth_time_seconds": 15,
  "crop_growth_time_seconds": 15,
  "iron_golem_spawn_enabled": true,
  "iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds": 5,
  "max_automatic_golems_per_area": 10,
  "animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled": true,
  "baby_growth_time_seconds": 15,
  "instant_growth_when_fed": true,
  "sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds": 5,
  "villager_emerald_drop_enabled": true,
  "villager_emerald_drop_chance": 0.4,
  "guaranteed_chicken_from_egg": true,
  "chicken_egg_interval_seconds": 20
}
```

### Configuration Options

| Option | Default | Description |
|--------|---------|-------------|
| `sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled` | `true` | Enable sugar cane placement/growth on soul sand |
| `sugar_cane_growth_interval_seconds` | `1` | Growth interval for sugar cane on soul sand (seconds) |
| `sapling_growth_time_seconds` | `15` | Time for saplings to grow into trees (seconds) |
| `crop_growth_time_seconds` | `15` | Time for crops to reach maturity (seconds) |
| `iron_golem_spawn_enabled` | `true` | Enable iron golem spawning from iron block villagers |
| `iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds` | `5` | Interval between golem spawn attempts (seconds) |
| `max_automatic_golems_per_area` | `10` | Maximum automatic golems per village area |
| `animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled` | `true` | Remove animal breeding cooldown |
| `baby_growth_time_seconds` | `15` | Time for baby animals to mature (seconds) |
| `instant_growth_when_fed` | `true` | Instantly grow baby animals when fed breeding item |
| `sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds` | `5` | Time for sheep to regrow wool (seconds) |
| `villager_emerald_drop_enabled` | `true` | Enable emerald drops from villager trades |
| `villager_emerald_drop_chance` | `0.4` | Chance (0.0-1.0) for emerald drop per trade |
| `guaranteed_chicken_from_egg` | `true` | Guarantee chicken spawn from thrown eggs |
| `chicken_egg_interval_seconds` | `20` | Interval for chicken egg laying (seconds) |

## Building from Source

Requirements:
- Java 21+
- Gradle 8.11+

```bash
./gradlew build
```

The compiled JAR will be in `build/libs/` and `dist/`.

## Test Server

A test server setup is included in the `test-server/` directory with scripts:

- `build.sh` - Build the mod and copy to test server
- `start-test-server.sh` - Start the test server
- `stop-test-server.sh` - Stop the test server

## Compatibility

- Minecraft: 1.21.4
- Fabric Loader: 0.16.9+
- Fabric API: 0.118.0+1.21.4
- Java: 21+

[0m$ [0msed -n '1,240p' src/main/java/pro/codeservermod/CoreServerMod.java; echo ====CONFIG; cat src/main/java/pro/codeservermod/Config.java; echo ====GOLEM; cat src/main/java/pro/codeservermod/handler/IronGolemHandler.java; echo ====WAKE; cat src/main/java/pro/codeservermod/mixin/VillagerWakeMixin.java
package pro.codeservermod;

import com.mojang.logging.LogUtils;
import net.fabricmc.api.DedicatedServerModInitializer;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.event.lifecycle.v1.ServerLifecycleEvents;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.event.lifecycle.v1.ServerTickEvents;
import net.fabricmc.loader.api.FabricLoader;
import net.minecraft.server.world.ServerWorld;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import pro.codeservermod.handler.*;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;

public class CoreServerMod implements DedicatedServerModInitializer {
    public static final String MOD_ID = "coreservermod";
    public static final Logger LOGGER = LogUtils.getLogger();
    
    public static Config CONFIG;
    public static Path CONFIG_PATH;

    @Override
    public void onInitializeServer() {
        LOGGER.info("Initializing CoreServerMod...");
        
        CONFIG_PATH = FabricLoader.getInstance().getConfigDir().resolve("coreservermod.json");
        loadConfig();
        
        registerEventHandlers();
        
        LOGGER.info("CoreServerMod initialized successfully!");
    }
    
    private void loadConfig() {
        try {
            if (!Files.exists(CONFIG_PATH)) {
                saveDefaultConfig();
            }
            String json = Files.readString(CONFIG_PATH);
            CONFIG = Config.fromJson(json);
            LOGGER.info("Configuration loaded from {}", CONFIG_PATH);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            LOGGER.error("Failed to load configuration, using defaults", e);
            CONFIG = Config.getDefaults();
        }
    }
    
    private void saveDefaultConfig() {
        try {
            Files.createDirectories(CONFIG_PATH.getParent());
            Files.writeString(CONFIG_PATH, Config.getDefaultJson());
            LOGGER.info("Created default configuration at {}", CONFIG_PATH);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            LOGGER.error("Failed to create default configuration", e);
        }
    }
    
    private void registerEventHandlers() {
        ServerLifecycleEvents.SERVER_STARTED.register(server -> {
            LOGGER.info("CoreServerMod: Server started, registering tick handlers");
            registerTickHandlers(server);
        });
        
        ServerLifecycleEvents.SERVER_STOPPING.register(server -> {
            LOGGER.info("CoreServerMod: Server stopping, cleaning up");
            cleanup();
        });
        
        ServerTickEvents.END_SERVER_TICK.register(server -> {
            if (CONFIG == null) return;
            
            ServerWorld overworld = server.getOverworld();
            if (overworld == null) return;
            
            SugarCaneHandler.tick(overworld);
            SaplingHandler.tick(overworld);
            IronGolemHandler.tick(overworld);
            CropGrowthHandler.tick(overworld);
            AnimalBreedingHandler.tick(overworld);
            BabyGrowthHandler.tick(overworld);
            SheepWoolHandler.tick(overworld);
            VillagerEmeraldHandler.tick(overworld);
            ChickenEggHandler.tick(overworld);
        });
    }
    
    private void registerTickHandlers(net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer server) {
        SugarCaneHandler.register(server);
        SaplingHandler.register(server);
        IronGolemHandler.register(server);
        CropGrowthHandler.register(server);
        AnimalBreedingHandler.register(server);
        BabyGrowthHandler.register(server);
        SheepWoolHandler.register(server);
        VillagerEmeraldHandler.register(server);
        ChickenEggHandler.register(server);
        ChickenSpawnHandler.register(server);
    }
    
    private void cleanup() {
        SugarCaneHandler.cleanup();
        SaplingHandler.cleanup();
        IronGolemHandler.cleanup();
        CropGrowthHandler.cleanup();
        AnimalBreedingHandler.cleanup();
        BabyGrowthHandler.cleanup();
        SheepWoolHandler.cleanup();
        VillagerEmeraldHandler.cleanup();
        ChickenEggHandler.cleanup();
        ChickenSpawnHandler.cleanup();
    }
}====CONFIG
package pro.codeservermod;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.google.gson.GsonBuilder;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;

public class Config {
    public boolean sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled = true;
    public int sugar_cane_growth_interval_seconds = 1;
    public int sapling_growth_time_seconds = 15;
    public int crop_growth_time_seconds = 15;
    public boolean iron_golem_spawn_enabled = true;
    public int iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds = 5;
    public int max_automatic_golems_per_area = 10;
    public boolean animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled = true;
    public int baby_growth_time_seconds = 15;
    public boolean instant_growth_when_fed = true;
    public int sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds = 5;
    public boolean villager_emerald_drop_enabled = true;
    public double villager_emerald_drop_chance = 0.4;
    public boolean guaranteed_chicken_from_egg = true;
    public int chicken_egg_interval_seconds = 20;

    private static final Gson GSON = new GsonBuilder().setPrettyPrinting().create();

    public static Config getDefaults() {
        return new Config();
    }

    public static String getDefaultJson() {
        return GSON.toJson(getDefaults());
    }

    public static Config fromJson(String json) {
        JsonObject obj = JsonParser.parseString(json).getAsJsonObject();
        Config config = new Config();
        
        config.sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled = getBoolean(obj, "sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled", true);
        config.sugar_cane_growth_interval_seconds = clampInt(getInt(obj, "sugar_cane_growth_interval_seconds", 1), 1, 3600);
        config.sapling_growth_time_seconds = clampInt(getInt(obj, "sapling_growth_time_seconds", 15), 1, 3600);
        config.crop_growth_time_seconds = clampInt(getInt(obj, "crop_growth_time_seconds", 15), 1, 3600);
        config.iron_golem_spawn_enabled = getBoolean(obj, "iron_golem_spawn_enabled", true);
        config.iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds = clampInt(getInt(obj, "iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds", 5), 1, 3600);
        config.max_automatic_golems_per_area = clampInt(getInt(obj, "max_automatic_golems_per_area", 10), 0, 100);
        config.animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled = getBoolean(obj, "animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled", true);
        config.baby_growth_time_seconds = clampInt(getInt(obj, "baby_growth_time_seconds", 15), 1, 3600);
        config.instant_growth_when_fed = getBoolean(obj, "instant_growth_when_fed", true);
        config.sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds = clampInt(getInt(obj, "sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds", 5), 1, 3600);
        config.villager_emerald_drop_enabled = getBoolean(obj, "villager_emerald_drop_enabled", true);
        config.villager_emerald_drop_chance = clampDouble(getDouble(obj, "villager_emerald_drop_chance", 0.4), 0.0, 1.0);
        config.guaranteed_chicken_from_egg = getBoolean(obj, "guaranteed_chicken_from_egg", true);
        config.chicken_egg_interval_seconds = clampInt(getInt(obj, "chicken_egg_interval_seconds", 20), 1, 3600);
        
        return config;
    }

    private static boolean getBoolean(JsonObject obj, String key, boolean defaultValue) {
        return obj.has(key) && !obj.get(key).isJsonNull() ? obj.get(key).getAsBoolean() : defaultValue;
    }

    private static int getInt(JsonObject obj, String key, int defaultValue) {
        return obj.has(key) && !obj.get(key).isJsonNull() ? obj.get(key).getAsInt() : defaultValue;
    }

    private static double getDouble(JsonObject obj, String key, double defaultValue) {
        return obj.has(key) && !obj.get(key).isJsonNull() ? obj.get(key).getAsDouble() : defaultValue;
    }

    private static int clampInt(int value, int min, int max) {
        return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
    }

    private static double clampDouble(double value, double min, double max) {
        return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
    }

    public String toJson() {
        return GSON.toJson(this);
    }

    public void save() {
        try {
            java.nio.file.Files.writeString(CoreServerMod.CONFIG_PATH, toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            CoreServerMod.LOGGER.error("Failed to save configuration", e);
        }
    }
}====GOLEM
package pro.codeservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.entity.passive.IronGolemEntity;
import net.minecraft.entity.passive.VillagerEntity;
import net.minecraft.server.world.ServerWorld;
import net.minecraft.util.math.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.util.math.Box;
import net.minecraft.util.math.Vec3d;
import net.minecraft.entity.SpawnReason;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedQueue;

public class IronGolemHandler {
    private static final Map<BlockPos, Long> VILLAGER_IRON_BLOCK_TIMES = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private static int tickCounter = 0;

    public static void register(net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer server) {
        // Initial setup
    }

    public static void scheduleGolemCheck(ServerWorld world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        // Check if the villager is on an iron block
        if (world.getBlockState(villagerPos.down()).isOf(Blocks.IRON_BLOCK)) {
            VILLAGER_IRON_BLOCK_TIMES.put(villagerPos.toImmutable(), world.getTime());
        }
    }

    public static void tick(ServerWorld world) {
        tickCounter++;
        int intervalTicks = 20 * pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds;
        
        if (tickCounter % intervalTicks != 0) return;
        
        if (!pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.iron_golem_spawn_enabled) return;

        // Clean up entries for villagers no longer on iron blocks
        for (Map.Entry<BlockPos, Long> entry : new HashMap<>(VILLAGER_IRON_BLOCK_TIMES).entrySet()) {
            BlockPos villagerPos = entry.getKey();
            
            if (!world.isChunkLoaded(villagerPos.getX() >> 4, villagerPos.getZ() >> 4)) continue;
            
            // Check if villager is still on iron block
            if (!world.getBlockState(villagerPos.down()).isOf(Blocks.IRON_BLOCK)) {
                VILLAGER_IRON_BLOCK_TIMES.remove(villagerPos);
                continue;
            }
            
            // Check for valid spawn position
            if (canSpawnGolem(world, villagerPos)) {
                spawnGolem(world, villagerPos);
            }
        }
    }

    private static boolean canSpawnGolem(ServerWorld world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        // Check if there's a valid spawn position near the villager
        for (int x = -16; x <= 16; x += 4) {
            for (int z = -16; z <= 16; z += 4) {
                for (int y = -4; y <= 4; y++) {
                    BlockPos checkPos = villagerPos.add(x, y, z);
                    if (isValidSpawnPosition(world, checkPos)) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static boolean isValidSpawnPosition(ServerWorld world, BlockPos pos) {
        // Check if position is air and block below is solid
        if (!world.getBlockState(pos).isAir()) return false;
        if (!world.getBlockState(pos.up()).isAir()) return false;
        if (!world.getBlockState(pos.down()).isSideSolidFullSquare(world, pos.down(), net.minecraft.util.math.Direction.UP)) return false;
        
        // Check not in liquid
        if (world.getBlockState(pos).getFluidState().isStill()) return false;
        if (world.getBlockState(pos.up()).getFluidState().isStill()) return false;
        
        // Check collision
        Box box = EntityType.IRON_GOLEM.getDimensions().getBoxAt(Vec3d.ofCenter(pos));
        Entity testEntity = EntityType.IRON_GOLEM.create(world, SpawnReason.TRIGGERED);
        if (testEntity == null || !world.isSpaceEmpty(testEntity, box)) return false;
        
        return true;
    }

    private static void spawnGolem(ServerWorld world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        for (int x = -16; x <= 16; x += 4) {
            for (int z = -16; z <= 16; z += 4) {
                for (int y = -4; y <= 4; y++) {
                    BlockPos spawnPos = villagerPos.add(x, y, z);
                    if (isValidSpawnPosition(world, spawnPos)) {
                        IronGolemEntity golem = EntityType.IRON_GOLEM.create(world, SpawnReason.TRIGGERED);
                        if (golem != null) {
                            golem.refreshPositionAndAngles(Vec3d.ofCenter(spawnPos), 0, 0);
                            golem.setPersistent();
                            world.spawnEntity(golem);
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        VILLAGER_IRON_BLOCK_TIMES.clear();
    }
}====WAKE
package pro.codeservermod.mixin;

import net.minecraft.entity.passive.VillagerEntity;
import net.minecraft.server.world.ServerWorld;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod;
import pro.codeservermod.handler.IronGolemHandler;

@Mixin(VillagerEntity.class)
public class VillagerWakeMixin {
    @Inject(method = "tick", at = @At("TAIL"))
    private void onTick(CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.iron_golem_spawn_enabled) return;
        
        VillagerEntity villager = (VillagerEntity) (Object) this;
        if (villager.getWorld() instanceof ServerWorld serverWorld) {
            // Check if villager is standing on iron block
            if (serverWorld.getBlockState(villager.getBlockPos().down()).isOf(net.minecraft.block.Blocks.IRON_BLOCK)) {
                IronGolemHandler.scheduleGolemCheck(serverWorld, villager.getBlockPos());
            }
        }
    }

public class AnimalBreedingHandler {
    private static final Map<Integer, Long> BRED_ENTITIES = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private static int tickCounter = 0;

    public static void register(net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer server) {
        // Setup
    }

    public static void onAnimalBred(ServerWorld world, AnimalEntity parent1, AnimalEntity parent2) {
        if (!pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled) return;
        
        // Reset breeding cooldown immediately
        BRED_ENTITIES.put(parent1.getId(), world.getTime());
        BRED_ENTITIES.put(parent2.getId(), world.getTime());
    }

    public static void tick(ServerWorld world) {
        tickCounter++;
        
        // Clean up old entries
        long currentTime = world.getTime();
        BRED_ENTITIES.entrySet().removeIf(entry -> currentTime - entry.getValue() > 24000); // 20 minutes
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        BRED_ENTITIES.clear();
    }

import net.minecraft.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.entity.passive.AnimalEntity;
import net.minecraft.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.server.world.ServerWorld;
import net.minecraft.util.Hand;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class BabyGrowthHandler {
    private static final Map<Integer, Long> BABY_ENTITIES = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private static final Set<Integer> FED_BABIES = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
    private static int tickCounter = 0;

    public static void register(net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer server) {
        // Setup
    }

    public static void trackBaby(ServerWorld world, Entity entity) {
        if (entity instanceof AnimalEntity animal && animal.isBaby()) {
            BABY_ENTITIES.put(entity.getId(), world.getTime());
        }
    }

    public static void onBabyFed(ServerWorld world, AnimalEntity baby, ItemStack stack) {
        if (!pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.instant_growth_when_fed) return;
        if (!baby.isBaby()) return;
        
        // Check if it's a valid breeding food for this animal
        if (baby.isBreedingItem(stack)) {
            FED_BABIES.add(baby.getId());
            baby.growUp(-24000);
        }
    }

    public static void tick(ServerWorld world) {
        tickCounter++;
        int intervalTicks = 20 * pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.baby_growth_time_seconds;
        
        if (tickCounter % intervalTicks != 0) return;
        
        long currentTime = world.getTime();
        
        for (Map.Entry<Integer, Long> entry : new java.util.HashMap<>(BABY_ENTITIES).entrySet()) {
            int entityId = entry.getKey();
            long spawnTime = entry.getValue();
            
            Entity entity = world.getEntityById(entityId);
            if (entity == null || !(entity instanceof AnimalEntity animal)) {
                BABY_ENTITIES.remove(entityId);
                FED_BABIES.remove(entityId);
                continue;
            }
            
            if (!animal.isBaby()) {
                BABY_ENTITIES.remove(entityId);
                FED_BABIES.remove(entityId);
                continue;
            }
            
            if (FED_BABIES.contains(entityId)) {
                BABY_ENTITIES.remove(entityId);
                FED_BABIES.remove(entityId);
                continue;
            }
            
            if (currentTime - spawnTime >= intervalTicks) {
                animal.growUp(-24000);
                BABY_ENTITIES.remove(entityId);
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        BABY_ENTITIES.clear();
        FED_BABIES.clear();
    }
}package pro.codeservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.entity.passive.ChickenEntity;

import net.minecraft.item.Items;

import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class ChickenEggHandler {
    private static final Map<Integer, Long> CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private static int tickCounter = 0;

    public static void register(net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer server) {
        // Setup
    }

    public static void scheduleEggLay(ServerWorld world, int chickenId) {
        long currentTime = world.getTime();
        Long lastLay = CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS.get(chickenId);
        
        if (lastLay == null) {
            CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS.put(chickenId, currentTime);
        }
    }

    public static void tick(ServerWorld world) {
        tickCounter++;
        int intervalTicks = 20 * pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.chicken_egg_interval_seconds;
        
        if (tickCounter % intervalTicks != 0) return;
        
        long currentTime = world.getTime();
        
        for (Map.Entry<Integer, Long> entry : new java.util.HashMap<>(CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS).entrySet()) {
            int chickenId = entry.getKey();
            long lastLay = entry.getValue();
            
            if (currentTime - lastLay >= intervalTicks) {
                net.minecraft.entity.Entity entity = world.getEntityById(chickenId);
                if (entity instanceof ChickenEntity chicken && !chicken.isBaby()) {
                    ItemStack egg = new ItemStack(Items.EGG, 1);
                    chicken.dropStack(world, egg);
                    CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS.put(chickenId, currentTime);
                } else if (entity == null || entity instanceof ChickenEntity chicken && chicken.isBaby()) {
                    CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS.remove(chickenId);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS.clear();
    }

import net.minecraft.block.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.block.CropBlock;
import net.minecraft.server.world.ServerWorld;
import net.minecraft.util.math.BlockPos;

import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class CropGrowthHandler {
    private static final Set<BlockPos> TRACKED_CROPS = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
    private static int tickCounter = 0;

    public static void register(net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer server) {
        // Could do initial scan
    }

    public static void trackCrop(ServerWorld world, BlockPos pos) {
        BlockState state = world.getBlockState(pos);
        if (state.getBlock() instanceof CropBlock) {
            TRACKED_CROPS.add(pos.toImmutable());
        }
    }

    public static void untrackCrop(BlockPos pos) {
        TRACKED_CROPS.remove(pos);
    }

    public static void tick(ServerWorld world) {
        tickCounter++;
        int intervalTicks = 20 * pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.crop_growth_time_seconds;
        
        if (tickCounter % intervalTicks != 0) return;
        
        for (BlockPos pos : new java.util.HashSet<>(TRACKED_CROPS)) {
            if (!world.isChunkLoaded(pos.getX() >> 4, pos.getZ() >> 4)) continue;
            
            BlockState state = world.getBlockState(pos);
            if (!(state.getBlock() instanceof CropBlock crop)) {
                TRACKED_CROPS.remove(pos);
                continue;
            }
            
            int maxAge = crop.getMaxAge();
            int currentAge = crop.getAge(state);
            
            if (currentAge < maxAge) {
                world.setBlockState(pos, crop.withAge(currentAge + 1));
            } else {
                TRACKED_CROPS.remove(pos);
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        TRACKED_CROPS.clear();
    }
}package pro.codeservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.entity.passive.IronGolemEntity;
import net.minecraft.entity.passive.VillagerEntity;
import net.minecraft.server.world.ServerWorld;
import net.minecraft.util.math.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.util.math.Box;
import net.minecraft.util.math.Vec3d;
import net.minecraft.entity.SpawnReason;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedQueue;

public class IronGolemHandler {
    private static final Map<BlockPos, Long> VILLAGER_IRON_BLOCK_TIMES = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private static int tickCounter = 0;

    public static void register(net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer server) {
        // Initial setup
    }

    public static void scheduleGolemCheck(ServerWorld world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        // Check if the villager is on an iron block
        if (world.getBlockState(villagerPos.down()).isOf(Blocks.IRON_BLOCK)) {
            VILLAGER_IRON_BLOCK_TIMES.put(villagerPos.toImmutable(), world.getTime());
        }
    }

    public static void tick(ServerWorld world) {
        tickCounter++;
        int intervalTicks = 20 * pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds;
        
        if (tickCounter % intervalTicks != 0) return;
        
        if (!pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.iron_golem_spawn_enabled) return;

        // Clean up entries for villagers no longer on iron blocks
        for (Map.Entry<BlockPos, Long> entry : new HashMap<>(VILLAGER_IRON_BLOCK_TIMES).entrySet()) {
            BlockPos villagerPos = entry.getKey();
            
            if (!world.isChunkLoaded(villagerPos.getX() >> 4, villagerPos.getZ() >> 4)) continue;
            
            // Check if villager is still on iron block
            if (!world.getBlockState(villagerPos.down()).isOf(Blocks.IRON_BLOCK)) {
                VILLAGER_IRON_BLOCK_TIMES.remove(villagerPos);
                continue;
            }
            
            // Check for valid spawn position
            if (canSpawnGolem(world, villagerPos)) {
                spawnGolem(world, villagerPos);
            }
        }
    }

    private static boolean canSpawnGolem(ServerWorld world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        // Check if there's a valid spawn position near the villager
        for (int x = -16; x <= 16; x += 4) {
            for (int z = -16; z <= 16; z += 4) {
                for (int y = -4; y <= 4; y++) {
                    BlockPos checkPos = villagerPos.add(x, y, z);
                    if (isValidSpawnPosition(world, checkPos)) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static boolean isValidSpawnPosition(ServerWorld world, BlockPos pos) {
        // Check if position is air and block below is solid
        if (!world.getBlockState(pos).isAir()) return false;
        if (!world.getBlockState(pos.up()).isAir()) return false;
        if (!world.getBlockState(pos.down()).isSideSolidFullSquare(world, pos.down(), net.minecraft.util.math.Direction.UP)) return false;
        
        // Check not in liquid
        if (world.getBlockState(pos).getFluidState().isStill()) return false;
        if (world.getBlockState(pos.up()).getFluidState().isStill()) return false;
        
        // Check collision
        Box box = EntityType.IRON_GOLEM.getDimensions().getBoxAt(Vec3d.ofCenter(pos));
        Entity testEntity = EntityType.IRON_GOLEM.create(world, SpawnReason.TRIGGERED);
        if (testEntity == null || !world.isSpaceEmpty(testEntity, box)) return false;
        
        return true;
    }

    private static void spawnGolem(ServerWorld world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        for (int x = -16; x <= 16; x += 4) {
            for (int z = -16; z <= 16; z += 4) {
                for (int y = -4; y <= 4; y++) {
                    BlockPos spawnPos = villagerPos.add(x, y, z);
                    if (isValidSpawnPosition(world, spawnPos)) {
                        IronGolemEntity golem = EntityType.IRON_GOLEM.create(world, SpawnReason.TRIGGERED);
                        if (golem != null) {
                            golem.refreshPositionAndAngles(Vec3d.ofCenter(spawnPos), 0, 0);
                            golem.setPersistent();
                            world.spawnEntity(golem);
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        VILLAGER_IRON_BLOCK_TIMES.clear();
    }
}package pro.codeservermod.handler;

    private static int tickCounter = 0;

    public static void register(net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer server) {
        // Setup
    }

    public static void scheduleRegrowth(ServerWorld world, int sheepId) {
        SHEARED_SHEEP.put(sheepId, world.getTime());
    }

    public static void tick(ServerWorld world) {
        tickCounter++;
        int intervalTicks = 20 * pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds;
        
        if (tickCounter % intervalTicks != 0) return;
        
        long currentTime = world.getTime();
        
        for (Map.Entry<Integer, Long> entry : new java.util.HashMap<>(SHEARED_SHEEP).entrySet()) {
            int sheepId = entry.getKey();
            long shearTime = entry.getValue();
            
            if (currentTime - shearTime >= intervalTicks) {
                net.minecraft.entity.Entity entity = world.getEntityById(sheepId);
                if (entity instanceof SheepEntity sheep) {
                    if (sheep.isSheared()) {
                        sheep.setSheared(false);
                        SHEARED_SHEEP.remove(sheepId);
                    }
                } else {
                    SHEARED_SHEEP.remove(sheepId);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        SHEARED_SHEEP.clear();
    }

import net.minecraft.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.server.world.ServerWorld;
import net.minecraft.util.math.BlockPos;

import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class SugarCaneHandler {
    private static final Set<BlockPos> TRACKED_POSITIONS = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
    private static final int GROWTH_INTERVAL_TICKS = 20;

    public static void register(net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer server) {
        // Initial scan could be added here if needed
    }

    public static void trackPosition(ServerWorld world, BlockPos pos) {
        if (world.getBlockState(pos.down()).isOf(Blocks.SOUL_SAND)) {
            TRACKED_POSITIONS.add(pos.toImmutable());
        }
    }

    public static void untrackPosition(BlockPos pos) {
        TRACKED_POSITIONS.remove(pos);
    }

    public static void tick(ServerWorld world) {
        if (TRACKED_POSITIONS.isEmpty()) return;
        
        for (BlockPos pos : new HashSet<>(TRACKED_POSITIONS)) {
            if (!world.isChunkLoaded(pos.getX() >> 4, pos.getZ() >> 4)) continue;
            
            if (!world.getBlockState(pos).isOf(Blocks.SUGAR_CANE)) {
                TRACKED_POSITIONS.remove(pos);
                continue;
            }
            
            if (!world.getBlockState(pos.down()).isOf(Blocks.SOUL_SAND)) {
                TRACKED_POSITIONS.remove(pos);
                continue;
            }
            
            int height = 1;
            while (height < 3 && world.getBlockState(pos.up(height)).isOf(Blocks.SUGAR_CANE)) {
                height++;
            }
            
            if (height < 3 && world.getBlockState(pos.up(height)).isAir()) {
                world.setBlockState(pos.up(height), Blocks.SUGAR_CANE.getDefaultState());
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        TRACKED_POSITIONS.clear();
    }

import net.minecraft.entity.passive.VillagerEntity;
import net.minecraft.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.server.world.ServerWorld;
import net.minecraft.util.math.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.util.math.random.Random;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class VillagerEmeraldHandler {
    private static final Map<Integer, Long> TRADE_COOLDOWNS = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private static final Random RANDOM = Random.create();

    public static void register(net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer server) {
        // Setup
    }

    public static void onTradeCompleted(ServerWorld world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        if (!pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.villager_emerald_drop_enabled) return;
        
        // Find villager at position
        VillagerEntity villager = null;
        for (VillagerEntity v : world.getEntitiesByType(net.minecraft.entity.EntityType.VILLAGER, 
                new net.minecraft.util.math.Box(villagerPos).expand(2), e -> true)) {
            if (v.getBlockPos().equals(villagerPos)) {
                villager = v;
                break;
            }
        }
        
        if (villager == null) return;
        
        int villagerId = villager.getId();
        long currentTime = world.getTime();
        
        // Prevent duplicate processing within 1 second (20 ticks)
        Long lastTrade = TRADE_COOLDOWNS.get(villagerId);
        if (lastTrade != null && currentTime - lastTrade < 20) {
            return;
        }
        
        TRADE_COOLDOWNS.put(villagerId, currentTime);
        
        // 40% chance to drop emerald
        if (RANDOM.nextDouble() < pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.villager_emerald_drop_chance) {
            ItemStack emerald = new ItemStack(Items.EMERALD, 1);
            villager.dropStack(world, emerald);
        }
    }

    public static void tick(ServerWorld world) {
        long currentTime = world.getTime();
        TRADE_COOLDOWNS.entrySet().removeIf(entry -> currentTime - entry.getValue() > 24000); // 20 minutes
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        TRADE_COOLDOWNS.clear();
    }

import net.minecraft.entity.passive.AnimalEntity;

import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;
import pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod;

@Mixin(AnimalEntity.class)
public class AnimalEntityMixin {
    @Inject(method = "canBreedWith", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void canBreed(AnimalEntity other, CallbackInfoReturnable<Boolean> cir) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled) return;
        
        AnimalEntity self = (AnimalEntity) (Object) this;
        if (self.isInLove() && other.isInLove()) {
            cir.setReturnValue(true);
        }
    }

    @Inject(method = "setLoveTicks", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void setBreedingAge(int age, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled) return;
        
        // Prevent setting negative love ticks (breeding cooldown)
        if (age < 0) {
            ci.cancel();
        }
    }

    @Inject(method = "randomTick", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void randomTick(BlockState state, ServerWorld world, BlockPos pos, Random random, CallbackInfo ci) {

        int maxAge = crop.getMaxAge();

                world.setBlockState(pos, crop.withAge(currentAge + 1));
            }
            ci.cancel();
        }

public class SaplingGeneratorMixin {
    @Inject(method = "generate", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void generate(ServerWorld world, ChunkGenerator chunkGenerator, BlockPos pos, BlockState state, Random random, CallbackInfoReturnable<Boolean> cir) {
        if (!(CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sapling_growth_time_seconds > 0)) return;
        
        if (random.nextInt(20 * CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sapling_growth_time_seconds) == 0) {
            boolean generated = ((SaplingGenerator) (Object) this).generate(world, chunkGenerator, pos, state, random);
            cir.setReturnValue(generated);
        } else {
            cir.setReturnValue(false);
        }
    }
}package pro.codeservermod.mixin;

@Mixin(AnimalEntity.class)
public class SheepEntityMixin {
    @Inject(method = "eat", at = @At("TAIL"))
    private void eat(CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds <= 0) return;
        
        AnimalEntity animal = (AnimalEntity) (Object) this;
        if (animal instanceof net.minecraft.entity.passive.SheepEntity sheep && animal.getWorld() instanceof ServerWorld serverWorld) {
            SheepWoolHandler.scheduleRegrowth(serverWorld, sheep.getId());
        }
    }

        if (CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds <= 0) return;
        
        SheepEntity sheep = (SheepEntity) (Object) this;

import net.minecraft.block.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.block.SugarCaneBlock;
import net.minecraft.server.world.ServerWorld;
import net.minecraft.util.math.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.util.math.random.Random;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;
import pro.codeservermod.Config;
import pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod;

@Mixin(SugarCaneBlock.class)
public class SugarCaneBlockMixin {
    @Inject(method = "canPlaceAt", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void canPlaceAt(BlockState state, net.minecraft.world.WorldView world, BlockPos pos, CallbackInfoReturnable<Boolean> cir) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled) return;
        
        BlockPos downPos = pos.down();
        BlockState downState = world.getBlockState(downPos);
        
        if (downState.isOf(Blocks.SOUL_SAND)) {
            cir.setReturnValue(true);
        }
    }

    @Inject(method = "randomTick", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void randomTick(BlockState state, ServerWorld world, BlockPos pos, Random random, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled) return;
        
        BlockPos downPos = pos.down();
        BlockState downState = world.getBlockState(downPos);
        
        if (downState.isOf(Blocks.SOUL_SAND)) {
            int maxHeight = 3;
            for (int i = 1; i <= maxHeight; i++) {
                BlockPos abovePos = pos.up(i);
                if (world.getBlockState(abovePos).isAir()) {
                    world.setBlockState(abovePos, state);
                    ci.cancel();
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}package pro.codeservermod.mixin;

@Mixin(EggEntity.class)
public class ThrownEggEntityMixin {
    @Inject(method = "onCollision", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void onCollision(HitResult hitResult, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.guaranteed_chicken_from_egg) return;
        
        EggEntity egg = (EggEntity) (Object) this;
        if (egg.getWorld() instanceof ServerWorld serverWorld) {
            ChickenSpawnHandler.spawnChickenFromEgg(serverWorld, egg.getBlockPos(), egg.getPos());
            ci.cancel();
        }
    }

import net.minecraft.entity.passive.VillagerEntity;
import net.minecraft.server.world.ServerWorld;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod;
import pro.codeservermod.handler.IronGolemHandler;

@Mixin(VillagerEntity.class)
public class VillagerWakeMixin {
    @Inject(method = "tick", at = @At("TAIL"))
    private void onTick(CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.iron_golem_spawn_enabled) return;
        
        VillagerEntity villager = (VillagerEntity) (Object) this;
        if (villager.getWorld() instanceof ServerWorld serverWorld) {
            // Check if villager is standing on iron block
            if (serverWorld.getBlockState(villager.getBlockPos().down()).isOf(net.minecraft.block.Blocks.IRON_BLOCK)) {
                IronGolemHandler.scheduleGolemCheck(serverWorld, villager.getBlockPos());
            }
        }
    }
}

[0m$ [0mfor f in build.gradle gradle.properties settings.gradle src/main/resources/fabric.mod.json; do echo ==== $f; curl -sL --max-time 30 https://raw.githubusercontent.com/FabricMC/fabric-example-mod/26.2/$f; done
==== build.gradle
plugins {
	id 'net.fabricmc.fabric-loom' version "${loom_version}"
	id 'maven-publish'
}

repositories {
	// Add repositories to retrieve artifacts from in here.
	// You should only use this when depending on other mods because
	// Loom adds the essential maven repositories to download Minecraft and libraries from automatically.
	// See https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/declaring_repositories.html
	// for more information about repositories.
}

loom {
	splitEnvironmentSourceSets()

	mods {
		"modid" {
			sourceSet sourceSets.main
			sourceSet sourceSets.client
		}
	}
}

dependencies {
	// To change the versions see the gradle.properties file
	minecraft "com.mojang:minecraft:${project.minecraft_version}"
	implementation "net.fabricmc:fabric-loader:${project.loader_version}"

	// Fabric API. This is technically optional, but you probably want it anyway.
	implementation "net.fabricmc.fabric-api:fabric-api:${project.fabric_api_version}"
}

processResources {
	def version = project.version
	inputs.property "version", version

	filesMatching("fabric.mod.json") {
		expand "version": version
	}
}

tasks.withType(JavaCompile).configureEach {
	it.options.release = 25
}

java {
	// Loom will automatically attach sourcesJar to a RemapSourcesJar task and to the "build" task
	// if it is present.
	// If you remove this line, sources will not be generated.
	withSourcesJar()

	sourceCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_25
	targetCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_25
}

jar {
	def projectName = project.name
	inputs.property "projectName", projectName

	from("LICENSE") {
		rename { "${it}_$projectName"}
	}
}

// configure the maven publication
publishing {
	publications {
		create("mavenJava", MavenPublication) {
			from components.java
		}
	}

	// See https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/publishing_maven.html for information on how to set up publishing.
	repositories {
		// Add repositories to publish to here.
		// Notice: This block does NOT have the same function as the block in the top level.
		// The repositories here will be used for publishing your artifact, not for
		// retrieving dependencies.
	}
}

# Dependencies

26.1 -> 26.2 Migration Primers Page Authors Porting to 26.2 26.2 ​ Guidelines for porting to Minecraft 26.2, the latest version of Minecraft. Minecraft is a game that's constantly evolving, with new versions changing the game in ways that affect modders. This article covers the general steps one might follow to update their mod to the newest stable version of Minecraft. INFO These docs discuss migrating from 26.1 to 26.2 . If you're looking for another migration, switch to the target version by using the dropdown in the top-right corner. Updating the Build Script ​ Start by updating your mod's gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties , gradle.properties , and build.gradle to the latest versions. If you run into trouble, consider referencing the Fabric Example Mod . Update Gradle to the latest version by running the following command: ./gradlew wrapper --gradle-version latest Bump Minecraft, Fabric Loader, Fabric Loom and Fabric API, either in gradle.properties (recommended) or in build.gradle . Find the recommended versions of the Fabric components on the Fabric Develop site . Refresh Gradle by using the refresh button in the top-right corner of IntelliJ IDEA. If this button is not visible, you can force caches to be cleared by running ./gradlew --refresh-dependencies . Updating the Code ​ After the build script has been updated to 26.2, you can now go through your mod and update any code that has changed to make it compatible with the snapshot. Fabric for Minecraft 26.2 on the Fabric blog contains a high-level explanation of the changes made to Fabric API in 26.2. Minecraft: Java Edition 26.2 on the Minecraft blog is the official overview of the features introduced in 26.2. Java Edition 26.2 on the Minecraft Wiki is an unofficial summary of the contents of the update. NeoForge's Minecraft 26.1 -> 26.2 Mod Migration Primer covers migrating from 26.1 to 26.2, focusing only on vanilla code changes. package net.fabricmc.fabric.api.event.lifecycle.v1;

import net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;

import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.event.Event;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.event.EventFactory;

/**
 * Contains events that are triggered on the server every tick.
 *
 * <p>A dedicated server may "pause" if no player is present for a
 * certain length of time (by default, 1 minute). See {@code pause-when-empty-seconds}
 * property in {@code server.properties}.
 * When the server is "paused", none of the events here will be invoked.
 */
public final class ServerTickEvents {
	private ServerTickEvents() {
	}

	/**
	 * Called at the start of the server tick.
	 *
	 * <p>When the dedicated server is "paused", this event is not invoked.
	 */
	public static final Event<StartTick> START_SERVER_TICK = EventFactory.createArrayBacked(StartTick.class, callbacks -> server -> {
		for (StartTick event : callbacks) {
			event.onStartTick(server);
		}
	});

	/**
	 * Called at the end of the server tick.
	 *
	 * <p>When the dedicated server is "paused", this event is not invoked.
	 */
	public static final Event<EndTick> END_SERVER_TICK = EventFactory.createArrayBacked(EndTick.class, callbacks -> server -> {
		for (EndTick event : callbacks) {
			event.onEndTick(server);
		}
	});

	/**
	 * Called at the start of a ServerLevel's tick.
	 *
	 * <p>When the dedicated server is "paused", this event is not invoked.
	 */
	public static final Event<StartLevelTick> START_LEVEL_TICK = EventFactory.createArrayBacked(StartLevelTick.class, callbacks -> level -> {
		for (StartLevelTick callback : callbacks) {
			callback.onStartTick(level);
		}
	});

	/**
	 * Called at the end of a ServerLevel's tick.
	 *
	 * <p>End of level tick may be used to start async computations for the next tick.
	 *
	 * <p>When the dedicated server is "paused", this event is not invoked.
	 */
	public static final Event<EndLevelTick> END_LEVEL_TICK = EventFactory.createArrayBacked(EndLevelTick.class, callbacks -> level -> {
		for (EndLevelTick callback : callbacks) {
			callback.onEndTick(level);
		}
	});

	@FunctionalInterface
	public interface StartTick {
		void onStartTick(MinecraftServer server);
	}

	@FunctionalInterface
	public interface EndTick {
		void onEndTick(MinecraftServer server);
	}

	@FunctionalInterface
	public interface StartLevelTick {
		void onStartTick(ServerLevel level);
	}

	@FunctionalInterface
	public interface EndLevelTick {
		void onEndTick(ServerLevel level);
	}
}

#!/bin/bash
# CoreServerMod Build Script
# Builds the Fabric mod JAR

set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"

echo "=== CoreServerMod Build Script ==="
echo "Working directory: $SCRIPT_DIR"

# Check for Gradle wrapper
if [ ! -f "./gradlew" ]; then
    echo "Generating Gradle wrapper..."
    gradle wrapper --gradle-version 8.10
fi

# Make gradlew executable
chmod +x ./gradlew

# Build the mod
echo "Building CoreServerMod..."
./gradlew clean build -x test

# Check if build was successful
JAR_PATH=$(find build/libs -name "CoreServerMod-*.jar" | head -1)
if [ -n "$JAR_PATH" ] && [ -f "$JAR_PATH" ]; then
    echo "Build successful! JAR: $JAR_PATH"
    
    # Copy to test server mods directory
    mkdir -p test-server/mods
    cp "$JAR_PATH" test-server/mods/
    echo "Copied JAR to test-server/mods/"
else
    echo "Build failed! JAR not found."
    exit 1
fi

echo "=== Build Complete ==="====
#!/bin/bash
# CoreServerMod Test Server Start Script
# Launches a dedicated Minecraft 1.21.4 Fabric server for testing

set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"

SERVER_DIR="$SCRIPT_DIR/test-server"
MODS_DIR="$SERVER_DIR/mods"
CONFIG_DIR="$SERVER_DIR/config"
WORLD_DIR="$SERVER_DIR/world"
LOGS_DIR="$SERVER_DIR/logs"

# Server configuration
MINECRAFT_VERSION="1.21.4"
FABRIC_LOADER_VERSION="0.16.9"
FABRIC_API_VERSION="0.118.0+1.21.4"
JAVA_VERSION="21"
SERVER_JAR="fabric-server-mc${MINECRAFT_VERSION}-loader${FABRIC_LOADER_VERSION}-launcher1.0.1.jar"
SERVER_URL="https://meta.fabricmc.net/v2/versions/loader/${MINECRAFT_VERSION}/${FABRIC_LOADER_VERSION}/1.0.1/server/jar"
FABRIC_API_URL="https://maven.fabricmc.net/net/fabricmc/fabric-api/fabric-api/${FABRIC_API_VERSION}/fabric-api-${FABRIC_API_VERSION}.jar"

# Memory settings
HEAP_MIN="2G"
HEAP_MAX="4G"

echo "=== CoreServerMod Test Server ==="
echo "Server directory: $SERVER_DIR"
echo "Minecraft version: $MINECRAFT_VERSION"
echo "Fabric Loader: $FABRIC_LOADER_VERSION"

# Create directories
mkdir -p "$MODS_DIR" "$CONFIG_DIR" "$WORLD_DIR" "$LOGS_DIR"

# Check for Java
if ! command -v java &> /dev/null; then
    echo "ERROR: Java not found. Please install Java $JAVA_VERSION or later."
    exit 1
fi

JAVA_VER=$(java -version 2>&1 | head -1 | cut -d'"' -f2 | cut -d'.' -f1)
if [ "$JAVA_VER" -lt "$JAVA_VERSION" ]; then
    echo "WARNING: Java version $JAVA_VER detected, recommended is $JAVA_VERSION+"
fi

# Download Fabric server JAR if not present
if [ ! -f "$SERVER_DIR/$SERVER_JAR" ]; then
    echo "Downloading Fabric server JAR..."
    cd "$SERVER_DIR"
    curl -L -o "$SERVER_JAR" "$SERVER_URL"
    echo "Download complete."
fi

# Download Fabric API if not present
FABRIC_API_JAR="fabric-api-${FABRIC_API_VERSION}.jar"
if [ ! -f "$MODS_DIR/$FABRIC_API_JAR" ]; then
    echo "Downloading Fabric API..."
    curl -L -o "$MODS_DIR/$FABRIC_API_JAR" "$FABRIC_API_URL"
    echo "Download complete."
fi

# Build mod if not present
MOD_JAR=$(find "$MODS_DIR" -name "CoreServerMod-*.jar" | head -1)
if [ -z "$MOD_JAR" ] || [ ! -f "$MOD_JAR" ]; then
    echo "CoreServerMod JAR not found. Building..."
    cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"
    ./build.sh
    MOD_JAR=$(find "$MODS_DIR" -name "CoreServerMod-*.jar" | head -1)
fi

# Check EULA
EULA_FILE="$SERVER_DIR/eula.txt"
if [ ! -f "$EULA_FILE" ]; then
    echo ""
    echo "=========================================="
    echo "MINECRAFT EULA AGREEMENT REQUIRED"
    echo "=========================================="
    echo "By starting this server, you agree to the Minecraft EULA:"
    echo "https://account.mojang.com/documents/minecraft_eula"
    echo ""
    read -p "Do you agree to the EULA? (yes/no): " EULA_AGREE
    if [ "$EULA_AGREE" = "yes" ] || [ "$EULA_AGREE" = "y" ]; then
        echo "eula=true" > "$EULA_FILE"
        echo "EULA accepted."
    else
        echo "EULA not accepted. Server cannot start."
        exit 1
    fi
fi

# Create server.properties if not present
PROPERTIES_FILE="$SERVER_DIR/server.properties"
if [ ! -f "$PROPERTIES_FILE" ]; then
    cat > "$PROPERTIES_FILE" << 'EOF'
# CoreServerMod Test Server Properties
server-ip=
server-port=25565
gamemode=survival
difficulty=normal
max-players=20
online-mode=false
allow-flight=false
view-distance=10
simulation-distance=10
motd=CoreServerMod Test Server
level-name=world
level-seed=
level-type=minecraft:normal
generate-structures=true
spawn-protection=0
max-tick-time=60000
EOF
    echo "Created default server.properties"
fi

# Start server

echo "Logs will be written to: $LOGS_DIR/latest.log"

    id "java"
    id "maven-publish"

group = "pro.coreservermod"

    toolchain.languageVersion.set(JavaLanguageVersion.of(25))
}

repositories {
    mavenCentral()
    maven { url "https://maven.fabricmc.net/" }
}

dependencies {
    minecraft "com.mojang:minecraft:26.2"
    implementation "net.fabricmc:fabric-loader:0.20.1"
    implementation "net.fabricmc.fabric-api:fabric-api:0.161.0+26.2"
}

    options.encoding = "UTF-8"
    options.release = 25
}

jar {
    manifest {
        attributes(
            "Fabric-Mod-Loading-Plugin": "pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod",
            "Mod-ID": "coreservermod",
            "Mod-Name": "CoreServerMod",
            "Mod-Version": version,
            "Mod-Authors": "promikailamin",
            "Mod-Description": "Minecraft Fabric Server Enhancement Mod - Accelerates crop/tree growth, automates mob behaviors, reduces waiting times",
            "Mod-Contact": "https://github.com/promikailamin/mcJava/issues/53",
            "Mod-License": "MIT",
            "Mod-Icon": "assets/coreservermod/icon.png",
            "Mixin-Configs": "coreservermod.mixins.json",
            "Implementation-Title": "CoreServerMod",
            "Implementation-Version": version,
            "Implementation-Vendor": "promikailamin"
        )
    }
}

publishing {
    publications {
        mavenJava(MavenPublication) {
            from components.java
        }
    }
    repositories {
        maven {
            url = uri("build/repo")
        }
    }
}

tasks.register("generateConfig") {
    doLast {
        def configDir = file("src/main/resources/config")
        configDir.mkdirs()
        def configFile = file("src/main/resources/config/coreservermod.json")
        if (!configFile.exists()) {
            configFile.write('''{
  "sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled": true,
  "sugar_cane_growth_interval_seconds": 1,
  "sapling_growth_time_seconds": 15,
  "crop_growth_time_seconds": 15,
  "iron_golem_spawn_enabled": true,
  "iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds": 5,
  "max_automatic_golems_per_area": 10,
  "animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled": true,
  "baby_growth_time_seconds": 15,
  "instant_growth_when_fed": true,
  "sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds": 5,
  "villager_emerald_drop_enabled": true,
  "villager_emerald_drop_chance": 0.4,
  "guaranteed_chicken_from_egg": true,
  "chicken_egg_interval_seconds": 20
}''')
        }
    }
}

tasks.named("processResources") {
    dependsOn("generateConfig")
}

--- start-test-server.sh ---
#!/bin/bash
# CoreServerMod Test Server Start Script
# Launches a dedicated Minecraft 1.21.4 Fabric server for testing

set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"

SERVER_DIR="$SCRIPT_DIR/test-server"
MODS_DIR="$SERVER_DIR/mods"
CONFIG_DIR="$SERVER_DIR/config"
WORLD_DIR="$SERVER_DIR/world"
LOGS_DIR="$SERVER_DIR/logs"

# Server configuration
MINECRAFT_VERSION="1.21.4"
FABRIC_LOADER_VERSION="0.16.9"
FABRIC_API_VERSION="0.118.0+1.21.4"
JAVA_VERSION="21"
SERVER_JAR="fabric-server-mc${MINECRAFT_VERSION}-loader${FABRIC_LOADER_VERSION}-launcher1.0.1.jar"
SERVER_URL="https://meta.fabricmc.net/v2/versions/loader/${MINECRAFT_VERSION}/${FABRIC_LOADER_VERSION}/1.0.1/server/jar"
FABRIC_API_URL="https://maven.fabricmc.net/net/fabricmc/fabric-api/fabric-api/${FABRIC_API_VERSION}/fabric-api-${FABRIC_API_VERSION}.jar"

# Memory settings
HEAP_MIN="2G"
HEAP_MAX="4G"

echo "=== CoreServerMod Test Server ==="
echo "Server directory: $SERVER_DIR"
echo "Minecraft version: $MINECRAFT_VERSION"
echo "Fabric Loader: $FABRIC_LOADER_VERSION"

# Create directories
mkdir -p "$MODS_DIR" "$CONFIG_DIR" "$WORLD_DIR" "$LOGS_DIR"

# Check for Java
if ! command -v java &> /dev/null; then
    echo "ERROR: Java not found. Please install Java $JAVA_VERSION or later."
    exit 1
fi

JAVA_VER=$(java -version 2>&1 | head -1 | cut -d'"' -f2 | cut -d'.' -f1)
if [ "$JAVA_VER" -lt "$JAVA_VERSION" ]; then
    echo "WARNING: Java version $JAVA_VER detected, recommended is $JAVA_VERSION+"
fi

# Download Fabric server JAR if not present
if [ ! -f "$SERVER_DIR/$SERVER_JAR" ]; then
    echo "Downloading Fabric server JAR..."
    cd "$SERVER_DIR"
    curl -L -o "$SERVER_JAR" "$SERVER_URL"
    echo "Download complete."
fi

# Download Fabric API if not present
FABRIC_API_JAR="fabric-api-${FABRIC_API_VERSION}.jar"
if [ ! -f "$MODS_DIR/$FABRIC_API_JAR" ]; then
    echo "Downloading Fabric API..."
    curl -L -o "$MODS_DIR/$FABRIC_API_JAR" "$FABRIC_API_URL"
    echo "Download complete."
fi

# Build mod if not present
MOD_JAR=$(find "$MODS_DIR" -name "CoreServerMod-*.jar" | head -1)
if [ -z "$MOD_JAR" ] || [ ! -f "$MOD_JAR" ]; then
    echo "CoreServerMod JAR not found. Building..."
    cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"
    ./build.sh
    MOD_JAR=$(find "$MODS_DIR" -name "CoreServerMod-*.jar" | head -1)
fi

# Check EULA
EULA_FILE="$SERVER_DIR/eula.txt"
if [ ! -f "$EULA_FILE" ]; then
    echo ""
    echo "=========================================="
    echo "MINECRAFT EULA AGREEMENT REQUIRED"
    echo "=========================================="
    echo "By starting this server, you agree to the Minecraft EULA:"
    echo "https://account.mojang.com/documents/minecraft_eula"
    echo ""
    read -p "Do you agree to the EULA? (yes/no): " EULA_AGREE
    if [ "$EULA_AGREE" = "yes" ] || [ "$EULA_AGREE" = "y" ]; then
        echo "eula=true" > "$EULA_FILE"
        echo "EULA accepted."
    else
        echo "EULA not accepted. Server cannot start."
        exit 1
    fi
fi

# Create server.properties if not present
PROPERTIES_FILE="$SERVER_DIR/server.properties"
if [ ! -f "$PROPERTIES_FILE" ]; then
    cat > "$PROPERTIES_FILE" << 'EOF'
# CoreServerMod Test Server Properties
server-ip=
server-port=25565
gamemode=survival
difficulty=normal
max-players=20
online-mode=false
allow-flight=false
view-distance=10
simulation-distance=10
motd=CoreServerMod Test Server
level-name=world
level-seed=
level-type=minecraft:normal
generate-structures=true
spawn-protection=0
max-tick-time=60000
EOF
    echo "Created default server.properties"
fi

# Start server
echo ""
echo "Starting CoreServerMod test server..."
echo "Logs will be written to: $LOGS_DIR/latest.log"
echo "Press Ctrl+C to stop the server"
echo ""

cd "$SERVER_DIR"
exec java -Xms${HEAP_MIN} -Xmx${HEAP_MAX} \
    -XX:+UseG1GC \
    -XX:+ParallelRefProcEnabled \
    -XX:MaxGCPauseMillis=200 \
    -XX:+UnlockExperimentalVMOptions \
    -XX:+DisableExplicitGC \

    -XX:G1NewSizePercent=30 \
    -XX:G1MaxNewSizePercent=40 \
    -XX:G1HeapRegionSize=8M \
    -XX:G1ReservePercent=20 \
    -XX:G1HeapWastePercent=5 \
    -XX:G1MixedGCCountTarget=4 \
    -XX:InitiatingHeapOccupancyPercent=15 \
    -XX:G1MixedGCLiveThresholdPercent=90 \
    -XX:G1RSetUpdatingPauseTimePercent=5 \
    -XX:SurvivorRatio=32 \
    -XX:+PerfDisableSharedMem \
    -XX:MaxTenuringThreshold=1 \
    -Dfml.readTimeout=120 \
    -jar "$SERVER_JAR" nogui 2>&1 | tee "$LOGS_DIR/latest.log"
--- build.sh ---
#!/bin/bash
# CoreServerMod Build Script
# Builds the Fabric mod JAR

set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"

echo "=== CoreServerMod Build Script ==="
echo "Working directory: $SCRIPT_DIR"

# Check for Gradle wrapper
if [ ! -f "./gradlew" ]; then
    echo "Generating Gradle wrapper..."
    gradle wrapper --gradle-version 8.10
fi

# Make gradlew executable
chmod +x ./gradlew

# Build the mod
echo "Building CoreServerMod..."
./gradlew clean build -x test

# Check if build was successful
JAR_PATH=$(find build/libs -name "CoreServerMod-*.jar" | head -1)
if [ -n "$JAR_PATH" ] && [ -f "$JAR_PATH" ]; then
    echo "Build successful! JAR: $JAR_PATH"
    
    # Copy to test server mods directory
    mkdir -p test-server/mods
    cp "$JAR_PATH" test-server/mods/
    echo "Copied JAR to test-server/mods/"
else
    echo "Build failed! JAR not found."
    exit 1
fi

echo "=== Build Complete ==="
--- README head ---
# CoreServerMod

A Fabric server-side mod for Minecraft Java Edition 1.21.4 that accelerates various game mechanics to reduce waiting times and automate repetitive tasks.

## Features

### 1. Sugar Cane on Soul Sand
- Allows sugar cane placement on soul sand
- Grows by one block approximately every second when planted on soul sand
- Preserves vanilla growth behavior on other valid substrates

### 2. Accelerated Tree Growth
- All vanilla saplings and propagules grow into their correct trees within approximately 15 seconds
- Requires valid environmental and space conditions

### 3. Iron Block Villager
- Villagers standing on iron blocks become special golem-spawning villagers
- Attempts to spawn exactly one iron golem every 100 server ticks (5 seconds) when a valid spawn position exists
- Does not require beds, village membership, gossip, panic, nearby mobs, professions, or other vanilla golem eligibility checks
- Moving off the iron block disables special behavior; standing on one again reactivates it with a fresh 5-second interval
- Other villagers retain vanilla behavior

### 4. Accelerated Crop Growth
- Applicable vanilla crops and plants grow to maturity within approximately 15 seconds
- Preserves necessary soil, light, water, and plant-specific requirements

### 5. Animal Breeding
- Removes breeding cooldown for supported breedable vanilla animals
- Allows repeated breeding whenever normal eligibility conditions are met
- Baby animals mature within approximately 15 seconds or instantly when fed once (configurable)

### 6. Sheep Wool Regrowth
- Sheep regrow wool and become shearable approximately every 5 seconds
- Preserves normal shearing drops and prevents duplicate drops

### 7. Villager Emerald Drops
- Villagers have a 40% chance to drop one emerald on successful trade
- Evaluates chance once per trade event and prevents duplicate rewards

### 8. Guaranteed Chicken from Eggs
- Every player-thrown chicken egg spawns exactly one chicken
- Bypasses vanilla random hatch chance without duplicate spawning

### 9. Chicken Egg Production
- Adult chickens produce one egg approximately every 20 seconds regardless of location or movement
- Baby chickens do not lay eggs

## Installation

1. Install Fabric Loader 0.16.9+ for Minecraft 1.21.4
2. Install Fabric API 0.118.0+1.21.4
3. Place `CoreServerMod-1.0.0.jar` in your server's `mods` folder
4. Start the server - a default configuration file will be generated at `config/coreservermod.json`

## Configuration

The mod generates a configuration file at `config/coreservermod.json` on first launch. All features can be toggled and intervals adjusted:

```json
{
  "sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled": true,
  "sugar_cane_growth_interval_seconds": 1,
  "sapling_growth_time_seconds": 15,
  "crop_growth_time_seconds": 15,
  "iron_golem_spawn_enabled": true,
  "iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds": 5,
  "max_automatic_golems_per_area": 10,
  "animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled": true,
  "baby_growth_time_seconds": 15,
  "instant_growth_when_fed": true,
  "sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds": 5,
  "villager_emerald_drop_enabled": true,
  "villager_emerald_drop_chance": 0.4,
  "guaranteed_chicken_from_egg": true,
  "chicken_egg_interval_seconds": 20
}
```

### Configuration Options

| Option | Default | Description |
|--------|---------|-------------|
| `sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled` | `true` | Enable sugar cane placement/growth on soul sand |
| `sugar_cane_growth_interval_seconds` | `1` | Growth interval for sugar cane on soul sand (seconds) |
| `sapling_growth_time_seconds` | `15` | Time for saplings to grow into trees (seconds) |
| `crop_growth_time_seconds` | `15` | Time for crops to reach maturity (seconds) |
| `iron_golem_spawn_enabled` | `true` | Enable iron golem spawning from iron block villagers |
| `iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds` | `5` | Interval between golem spawn attempts (seconds) |
| `max_automatic_golems_per_area` | `10` | Maximum automatic golems per village area |
| `animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled` | `true` | Remove animal breeding cooldown |
| `baby_growth_time_seconds` | `15` | Time for baby animals to mature (seconds) |
| `instant_growth_when_fed` | `true` | Instantly grow baby animals when fed breeding item |
| `sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds` | `5` | Time for sheep to regrow wool (seconds) |
| `villager_emerald_drop_enabled` | `true` | Enable emerald drops from villager trades |
| `villager_emerald_drop_chance` | `0.4` | Chance (0.0-1.0) for emerald drop per trade |
| `guaranteed_chicken_from_egg` | `true` | Guarantee chicken spawn from thrown eggs |
| `chicken_egg_interval_seconds` | `20` | Interval for chicken egg laying (seconds) |

## Building from Source

Requirements:
- Java 21+
- Gradle 8.11+

```bash
./gradlew build
```

The compiled JAR will be in `build/libs/` and `dist/`.

## Test Server

A test server setup is included in the `test-server/` directory with scripts:

- `build.sh` - Build the mod and copy to test server
- `start-test-server.sh` - Start the test server
- `stop-test-server.sh` - Stop the test server

## Compatibility

- Minecraft: 1.21.4
- Fabric Loader: 0.16.9+
- Fabric API: 0.118.0+1.21.4
- Java: 21+

package pro.coreservermod;

import net.fabricmc.api.DedicatedServerModInitializer;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.event.lifecycle.v1.ServerLifecycleEvents;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.event.lifecycle.v1.ServerTickEvents;
import net.fabricmc.loader.api.FabricLoader;
import net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import pro.coreservermod.handler.AnimalBreedingHandler;
import pro.coreservermod.handler.BabyGrowthHandler;
import pro.coreservermod.handler.ChickenEggHandler;
import pro.coreservermod.handler.ChickenSpawnHandler;
import pro.coreservermod.handler.CropGrowthHandler;
import pro.coreservermod.handler.IronGolemHandler;
import pro.coreservermod.handler.SaplingHandler;
import pro.coreservermod.handler.SheepWoolHandler;
import pro.coreservermod.handler.SugarCaneHandler;
import pro.coreservermod.handler.VillagerEmeraldHandler;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;

public class CoreServerMod implements DedicatedServerModInitializer {
    public static final String MOD_ID = "coreservermod";
    public static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(MOD_ID);

    public static Config CONFIG;
    public static Path CONFIG_PATH;

    @Override
    public void onInitializeServer() {
        LOGGER.info("Initializing CoreServerMod...");

        CONFIG_PATH = FabricLoader.getInstance().getConfigDir().resolve("coreservermod.json");
        loadConfig();

        registerEventHandlers();

        LOGGER.info("CoreServerMod initialized successfully!");
    }

    private void loadConfig() {
        try {
            if (!Files.exists(CONFIG_PATH)) {
                saveDefaultConfig();
            }
            String json = Files.readString(CONFIG_PATH);
            CONFIG = Config.fromJson(json);
            LOGGER.info("Configuration loaded from {}", CONFIG_PATH);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            LOGGER.error("Failed to load configuration, using defaults", e);
            CONFIG = Config.getDefaults();
        }
    }

    private void saveDefaultConfig() {
        try {
            Files.createDirectories(CONFIG_PATH.getParent());
            Files.writeString(CONFIG_PATH, Config.getDefaultJson());
            LOGGER.info("Created default configuration at {}", CONFIG_PATH);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            LOGGER.error("Failed to create default configuration", e);
        }
    }

    private void registerEventHandlers() {
        ServerLifecycleEvents.SERVER_STARTED.register(server -> {
            LOGGER.info("CoreServerMod: Server started, registering tick handlers");
            registerTickHandlers(server);
        });

        ServerLifecycleEvents.SERVER_STOPPING.register(server -> {
            LOGGER.info("CoreServerMod: Server stopping, cleaning up");
            cleanup();
        });

        ServerTickEvents.END_SERVER_TICK.register(server -> {
            if (CONFIG == null) return;

            for (ServerLevel level : server.getAllLevels()) {
                SugarCaneHandler.tick(level);
                SaplingHandler.tick(level);
                IronGolemHandler.tick(level);
                CropGrowthHandler.tick(level);
                AnimalBreedingHandler.tick(level);
                BabyGrowthHandler.tick(level);
                SheepWoolHandler.tick(level);
                VillagerEmeraldHandler.tick(level);
                ChickenEggHandler.tick(level);
            }
        });
    }

    private void registerTickHandlers(MinecraftServer server) {
        SugarCaneHandler.register(server);
        SaplingHandler.register(server);
        IronGolemHandler.register(server);
        CropGrowthHandler.register(server);
        AnimalBreedingHandler.register(server);
        BabyGrowthHandler.register(server);
        SheepWoolHandler.register(server);
        VillagerEmeraldHandler.register(server);
        ChickenEggHandler.register(server);
        ChickenSpawnHandler.register(server);
    }

    private void cleanup() {
        SugarCaneHandler.cleanup();
        SaplingHandler.cleanup();
        IronGolemHandler.cleanup();
        CropGrowthHandler.cleanup();
        AnimalBreedingHandler.cleanup();
        BabyGrowthHandler.cleanup();
        SheepWoolHandler.cleanup();
        VillagerEmeraldHandler.cleanup();
        ChickenEggHandler.cleanup();
        ChickenSpawnHandler.cleanup();
    }
}

package pro.coreservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntitySpawnReason;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityTypes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.golem.IronGolem;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.npc.villager.Villager;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;

import java.util.List;

public class IronGolemHandler {
    public static final String CREATE_VILLAGER_TAG = "CreateVillager";
    private static final Component CREATE_VILLAGER_NAME = Component.literal(CREATE_VILLAGER_TAG);
    private static final int VILLAGER_CHECK_INTERVAL_TICKS = 20 * 10;
    private static final int GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS = 16;

    public static void register(MinecraftServer server) {
        // Initial setup
    }

    public static void tick(ServerLevel world) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.iron_golem_spawn_enabled) return;

        long gameTime = world.getGameTime();

        if (gameTime % VILLAGER_CHECK_INTERVAL_TICKS == 0) {
            updateCreateVillagers(world);
        }

        int intervalTicks = 20 * CoreServerMod.CONFIG.iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds;
        if (gameTime % intervalTicks == 0) {
            spawnGolemsForCreateVillagers(world);
        }
    }

    private static void updateCreateVillagers(ServerLevel world) {
        for (Villager villager : world.getEntities(EntityTypes.VILLAGER, villager -> true)) {
            boolean onIronBlock = isStandingOnIronBlock(world, villager);
            boolean hasTag = villager.entityTags().contains(CREATE_VILLAGER_TAG);

            if (onIronBlock) {
                if (!hasTag) {
                    villager.addTag(CREATE_VILLAGER_TAG);
                }
                if (!CREATE_VILLAGER_NAME.equals(villager.getCustomName())) {
                    villager.setCustomName(CREATE_VILLAGER_NAME);
                    villager.setCustomNameVisible(true);
                }
            } else if (hasTag) {
                villager.removeTag(CREATE_VILLAGER_TAG);
                if (CREATE_VILLAGER_NAME.equals(villager.getCustomName())) {
                    villager.setCustomName(null);
                    villager.setCustomNameVisible(false);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private static void spawnGolemsForCreateVillagers(ServerLevel world) {
        for (Villager villager : world.getEntities(EntityTypes.VILLAGER, villager -> true)) {
            if (!villager.entityTags().contains(CREATE_VILLAGER_TAG)) continue;
            if (!isStandingOnIronBlock(world, villager)) continue;
            if (hasReachedGolemLimit(world, villager.blockPosition())) continue;
            if (canSpawnGolem(world, villager.blockPosition())) {
                spawnGolem(world, villager.blockPosition());
            }
        }
    }

    private static boolean isStandingOnIronBlock(ServerLevel world, Villager villager) {
        BlockPos below = villager.blockPosition().below();
        return world.isLoaded(below) && world.getBlockState(below).is(Blocks.IRON_BLOCK);
    }

    private static boolean hasReachedGolemLimit(ServerLevel world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        int max = CoreServerMod.CONFIG.max_automatic_golems_per_area;
        if (max <= 0) return false;

        List<IronGolem> nearby = world.getEntities(
                EntityTypes.IRON_GOLEM,
                new AABB(villagerPos).inflate(GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS),
                golem -> true
        );
        return nearby.size() >= max;
    }

    private static boolean canSpawnGolem(ServerLevel world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        for (int x = -GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; x <= GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; x += 4) {
            for (int z = -GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; z <= GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; z += 4) {
                for (int y = -4; y <= 4; y++) {
                    BlockPos checkPos = villagerPos.offset(x, y, z);
                    if (isValidSpawnPosition(world, checkPos)) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static boolean isValidSpawnPosition(ServerLevel world, BlockPos pos) {
        BlockState state = world.getBlockState(pos);
        BlockState above = world.getBlockState(pos.above());
        BlockState below = world.getBlockState(pos.below());

        if (!state.isAir() || !above.isAir()) return false;
        if (!below.isFaceSturdy(world, pos.below(), Direction.UP)) return false;

        if (!state.getFluidState().isEmpty() || !above.getFluidState().isEmpty()) return false;

        AABB box = EntityTypes.IRON_GOLEM.getDimensions()
                .makeBoundingBox(Vec3.atCenterOf(pos));
        return world.noCollision(box);
    }

    private static void spawnGolem(ServerLevel world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        for (int x = -GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; x <= GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; x += 4) {
            for (int z = -GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; z <= GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; z += 4) {
                for (int y = -4; y <= 4; y++) {
                    BlockPos spawnPos = villagerPos.offset(x, y, z);
                    if (isValidSpawnPosition(world, spawnPos)) {
                        IronGolem golem = EntityTypes.IRON_GOLEM.create(world, EntitySpawnReason.TRIGGERED);
                        if (golem != null) {
                            golem.snapTo(Vec3.atCenterOf(spawnPos), 0.0F, 0.0F);
                            golem.setPersistenceRequired();
                            world.addFreshEntity(golem);
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        // No cached state to clean up.
    }
}
