--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/mixin/AnimalEntityMixin.java
package pro.coreservermod.mixin;

import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.AgeableMob;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.Animal;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;

@Mixin(Animal.class)
public class AnimalEntityMixin {
    @Inject(method = "canMate", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void canMate(Animal other, CallbackInfoReturnable<Boolean> cir) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled) return;

        Animal self = (Animal) (Object) this;
        if (self.isInLove() && other.isInLove()) {
            cir.setReturnValue(true);
        }
    }

    @Inject(method = "finalizeSpawnChildFromBreeding", at = @At("TAIL"))
    private void clearBreedingCooldown(ServerLevel world, Animal other, AgeableMob child, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled) return;

        Animal self = (Animal) (Object) this;
        self.setAge(0);
        other.setAge(0);
    }
}
--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/mixin/ChickenEntityMixin.java
package pro.coreservermod.mixin;

import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.chicken.Chicken;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;
import pro.coreservermod.handler.ChickenEggHandler;

@Mixin(Entity.class)
public class ChickenEntityMixin {
    @Inject(method = "tick", at = @At("TAIL"))
    private void onEntityTick(CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (CoreServerMod.CONFIG.chicken_egg_interval_seconds <= 0) return;

        Entity entity = (Entity) (Object) this;
        if (entity instanceof Chicken chicken
                && entity.level() instanceof ServerLevel serverLevel
                && !chicken.isBaby()) {
            ChickenEggHandler.scheduleEggLay(serverLevel, chicken.getId());
        }
    }
}
--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/mixin/CropBlockMixin.java
package pro.coreservermod.mixin;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.CropBlock;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;

@Mixin(CropBlock.class)
public class CropBlockMixin {
    @Inject(method = "randomTick", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void randomTick(BlockState state, ServerLevel world, BlockPos pos, RandomSource random, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (!(CoreServerMod.CONFIG.crop_growth_time_seconds > 0)) return;

        CropBlock crop = (CropBlock) (Object) this;
        int maxAge = crop.getMaxAge();
        int currentAge = crop.getAge(state);

        if (currentAge < maxAge) {
            if (random.nextInt(20 * CoreServerMod.CONFIG.crop_growth_time_seconds) == 0) {
                world.setBlockAndUpdate(pos, crop.getStateForAge(currentAge + 1));
            }
            ci.cancel();
        }
    }
}
--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/mixin/SaplingGeneratorMixin.java
package pro.coreservermod.mixin;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.grower.TreeGrower;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ChunkGenerator;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;

@Mixin(TreeGrower.class)
public class SaplingGeneratorMixin {
    @Inject(method = "growTree", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void growTree(ServerLevel world, ChunkGenerator chunkGenerator, BlockPos pos, BlockState state,
                          RandomSource random, CallbackInfoReturnable<Boolean> cir) {
        if (!(CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sapling_growth_time_seconds > 0)) return;

        if (random.nextInt(20 * CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sapling_growth_time_seconds) != 0) {
            cir.setReturnValue(false);
        }
    }
}
--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/mixin/SheepShearedMixin.java
package pro.coreservermod.mixin;

import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.sheep.Sheep;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;
import pro.coreservermod.handler.SheepWoolHandler;

@Mixin(Sheep.class)
public class SheepShearedMixin {
    @Inject(method = "setSheared", at = @At("TAIL"))
    private void onSheared(boolean sheared, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds <= 0) return;

        Sheep sheep = (Sheep) (Object) this;
        if (sheep.level() instanceof ServerLevel serverLevel && sheared) {
            SheepWoolHandler.scheduleRegrowth(serverLevel, sheep.getId());
        }
    }
}
--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/mixin/SugarCaneBlockMixin.java
package pro.coreservermod.mixin;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.level.LevelReader;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.SugarCaneBlock;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;

@Mixin(SugarCaneBlock.class)
public class SugarCaneBlockMixin {
    @Inject(method = "canSurvive", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void canSurvive(BlockState state, LevelReader world, BlockPos pos, CallbackInfoReturnable<Boolean> cir) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled) return;

        if (world.getBlockState(pos.below()).is(Blocks.SOUL_SAND)) {
            cir.setReturnValue(true);
        }
    }

    @Inject(method = "randomTick", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void randomTick(BlockState state, ServerLevel world, BlockPos pos, RandomSource random, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled) return;

        if (world.getBlockState(pos.below()).is(Blocks.SOUL_SAND)) {
            int maxHeight = 3;
            for (int i = 1; i <= maxHeight; i++) {
                BlockPos abovePos = pos.above(i);
                if (world.getBlockState(abovePos).isAir()) {
                    world.setBlockAndUpdate(abovePos, state);
                    ci.cancel();
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/mixin/ThrownEggEntityMixin.java
package pro.coreservermod.mixin;

import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.projectile.throwableitemprojectile.ThrownEgg;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;
import pro.coreservermod.handler.ChickenSpawnHandler;

@Mixin(ThrownEgg.class)
public class ThrownEggEntityMixin {
    @Inject(method = "onHit", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void onHit(HitResult hitResult, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.guaranteed_chicken_from_egg) return;

        ThrownEgg egg = (ThrownEgg) (Object) this;
        if (egg.level() instanceof ServerLevel serverLevel) {
            ChickenSpawnHandler.spawnChickenFromEgg(serverLevel, egg.blockPosition(), egg.position());
            egg.discard();
            ci.cancel();
        }
    }
}
--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/mixin/VillagerTradeMixin.java
package pro.coreservermod.mixin;

import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.npc.villager.Villager;
import net.minecraft.world.item.trading.MerchantOffer;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;
import pro.coreservermod.handler.VillagerEmeraldHandler;

@Mixin(Villager.class)
public class VillagerTradeMixin {
    @Inject(method = "rewardTradeXp", at = @At("TAIL"))
    private void onTrade(MerchantOffer offer, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.villager_emerald_drop_enabled) return;

        Villager villager = (Villager) (Object) this;
        if (villager.level() instanceof ServerLevel serverLevel) {
            VillagerEmeraldHandler.onTradeCompleted(serverLevel, villager.blockPosition());
        }
    }
}

package pro.coreservermod;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.google.gson.GsonBuilder;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;

public class Config {
    public boolean sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled = true;
    public int sugar_cane_growth_interval_seconds = 1;
    public int sapling_growth_time_seconds = 15;
    public int crop_growth_time_seconds = 15;
    public boolean iron_golem_spawn_enabled = true;
    public int iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds = 5;
    public int max_automatic_golems_per_area = 10;
    public boolean animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled = true;
    public int baby_growth_time_seconds = 15;
    public boolean instant_growth_when_fed = true;
    public int sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds = 5;
    public boolean villager_emerald_drop_enabled = true;
    public double villager_emerald_drop_chance = 0.4;
    public boolean guaranteed_chicken_from_egg = true;
    public int chicken_egg_interval_seconds = 20;

    private static final Gson GSON = new GsonBuilder().setPrettyPrinting().create();

    public static Config getDefaults() {
        return new Config();
    }

    public static String getDefaultJson() {
        return GSON.toJson(getDefaults());
    }

    public static Config fromJson(String json) {
        JsonObject obj = JsonParser.parseString(json).getAsJsonObject();
        Config config = new Config();

        config.sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled = getBoolean(obj, "sugar_cane_soul_sand_enabled", true);
        config.sugar_cane_growth_interval_seconds = clampInt(getInt(obj, "sugar_cane_growth_interval_seconds", 1), 1, 3600);
        config.sapling_growth_time_seconds = clampInt(getInt(obj, "sapling_growth_time_seconds", 15), 1, 3600);
        config.crop_growth_time_seconds = clampInt(getInt(obj, "crop_growth_time_seconds", 15), 1, 3600);
        config.iron_golem_spawn_enabled = getBoolean(obj, "iron_golem_spawn_enabled", true);
        config.iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds = clampInt(getInt(obj, "iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds", 5), 1, 3600);
        config.max_automatic_golems_per_area = clampInt(getInt(obj, "max_automatic_golems_per_area", 10), 0, 100);
        config.animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled = getBoolean(obj, "animal_breeding_cooldown_disabled", true);
        config.baby_growth_time_seconds = clampInt(getInt(obj, "baby_growth_time_seconds", 15), 1, 3600);
        config.instant_growth_when_fed = getBoolean(obj, "instant_growth_when_fed", true);
        config.sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds = clampInt(getInt(obj, "sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds", 5), 1, 3600);
        config.villager_emerald_drop_enabled = getBoolean(obj, "villager_emerald_drop_enabled", true);
        config.villager_emerald_drop_chance = clampDouble(getDouble(obj, "villager_emerald_drop_chance", 0.4), 0.0, 1.0);
        config.guaranteed_chicken_from_egg = getBoolean(obj, "guaranteed_chicken_from_egg", true);
        config.chicken_egg_interval_seconds = clampInt(getInt(obj, "chicken_egg_interval_seconds", 20), 1, 3600);

        return config;
    }

    private static boolean getBoolean(JsonObject obj, String key, boolean defaultValue) {
        return obj.has(key) && !obj.get(key).isJsonNull() ? obj.get(key).getAsBoolean() : defaultValue;
    }

    private static int getInt(JsonObject obj, String key, int defaultValue) {
        return obj.has(key) && !obj.get(key).isJsonNull() ? obj.get(key).getAsInt() : defaultValue;
    }

    private static double getDouble(JsonObject obj, String key, double defaultValue) {
        return obj.has(key) && !obj.get(key).isJsonNull() ? obj.get(key).getAsDouble() : defaultValue;
    }

    private static int clampInt(int value, int min, int max) {
        return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
    }

    private static double clampDouble(double value, double min, double max) {
        return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
    }

    public String toJson() {
        return GSON.toJson(this);
    }

    public void save() {
        try {
            java.nio.file.Files.writeString(CoreServerMod.CONFIG_PATH, toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            CoreServerMod.LOGGER.error("Failed to save configuration", e);
        }
    }
}

--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/handler/BabyGrowthHandler.java
package pro.coreservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.Animal;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;

import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class BabyGrowthHandler {
    private static final Map<Integer, Long> BABY_ENTITIES = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private static final Set<Integer> FED_BABIES = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();

    public static void register(MinecraftServer server) {
        // Initial setup
    }

    public static void trackBaby(ServerLevel world, Entity entity) {
        if (entity instanceof Animal animal && animal.isBaby()) {
            BABY_ENTITIES.putIfAbsent(entity.getId(), world.getGameTime());
        }
    }

    public static void onBabyFed(ServerLevel world, Animal baby, ItemStack stack) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.instant_growth_when_fed) return;
        if (!baby.isBaby()) return;

        if (baby.isFood(stack)) {
            FED_BABIES.add(baby.getId());
            baby.setAge(0);
        }
    }

    public static void tick(ServerLevel world) {
        int intervalTicks = 20 * CoreServerMod.CONFIG.baby_growth_time_seconds;
        if (world.getGameTime() % intervalTicks != 0) return;

        long currentTime = world.getGameTime();
        Set<Integer> presentBabies = new HashSet<>();

        for (Entity entity : world.getAllEntities()) {
            if (!(entity instanceof Animal animal) || !animal.isBaby()) continue;

            int entityId = animal.getId();
            presentBabies.add(entityId);
            long firstSeen = BABY_ENTITIES.computeIfAbsent(entityId, id -> currentTime);

            if (FED_BABIES.remove(entityId) || currentTime - firstSeen >= intervalTicks) {
                animal.setAge(0);
                BABY_ENTITIES.remove(entityId);
            }
        }

        BABY_ENTITIES.keySet().retainAll(presentBabies);
        FED_BABIES.removeIf(id -> !presentBabies.contains(id));
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        BABY_ENTITIES.clear();
        FED_BABIES.clear();
    }
}
--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/handler/ChickenEggHandler.java
package pro.coreservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.chicken.Chicken;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class ChickenEggHandler {
    private static final Map<Integer, Long> CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public static void register(MinecraftServer server) {
        // Initial setup
    }

    public static void scheduleEggLay(ServerLevel world, int chickenId) {
        CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS.putIfAbsent(chickenId, world.getGameTime());
    }

    public static void tick(ServerLevel world) {
        int intervalTicks = 20 * CoreServerMod.CONFIG.chicken_egg_interval_seconds;
        if (world.getGameTime() % intervalTicks != 0) return;

        long currentTime = world.getGameTime();

        for (Map.Entry<Integer, Long> entry : new java.util.HashMap<>(CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS).entrySet()) {
            int chickenId = entry.getKey();
            long lastLay = entry.getValue();

            if (currentTime - lastLay >= intervalTicks) {
                Entity entity = world.getEntity(chickenId);
                if (entity instanceof Chicken chicken && !chicken.isBaby()) {
                    ItemStack egg = new ItemStack(Items.EGG, 1);
                    chicken.spawnAtLocation(world, egg);
                    CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS.put(chickenId, currentTime);
                } else if (entity == null || entity instanceof Chicken chicken && chicken.isBaby()) {
                    CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS.remove(chickenId);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS.clear();
    }
}

package pro.coreservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;

    public static void register(MinecraftServer server) {
        // Initial setup
    }

    public static void spawnChickenFromEgg(ServerLevel world, BlockPos pos, Vec3 exactPos) {
        Chicken chicken = EntityTypes.CHICKEN.create(world, EntitySpawnReason.TRIGGERED);
        if (chicken != null) {
            chicken.snapTo(exactPos, 0.0F, 0.0F);
            chicken.setPersistenceRequired();
            world.addFreshEntity(chicken);
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        // Nothing to clean up
    }
}
--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/handler/CropGrowthHandler.java
package pro.coreservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.CropBlock;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;

import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class CropGrowthHandler {
    private static final Set<BlockPos> TRACKED_CROPS = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();

    public static void register(MinecraftServer server) {
        // Could do initial scan
    }

    public static void trackCrop(ServerLevel world, BlockPos pos) {
        BlockState state = world.getBlockState(pos);
        if (state.getBlock() instanceof CropBlock) {
            TRACKED_CROPS.add(pos.immutable());
        }
    }

    public static void untrackCrop(BlockPos pos) {
        TRACKED_CROPS.remove(pos);
    }

    public static void tick(ServerLevel world) {
        if (TRACKED_CROPS.isEmpty()) return;

        int intervalTicks = 20 * CoreServerMod.CONFIG.crop_growth_time_seconds;
        if (world.getGameTime() % intervalTicks != 0) return;

        for (BlockPos pos : new java.util.HashSet<>(TRACKED_CROPS)) {
            if (!world.isLoaded(pos)) continue;

            BlockState state = world.getBlockState(pos);
            if (!(state.getBlock() instanceof CropBlock crop)) {
                TRACKED_CROPS.remove(pos);
                continue;
            }

            int maxAge = crop.getMaxAge();
            int currentAge = crop.getAge(state);

            if (currentAge < maxAge) {
                world.setBlockAndUpdate(pos, crop.getStateForAge(currentAge + 1));
            } else {
                TRACKED_CROPS.remove(pos);
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        TRACKED_CROPS.clear();
    }
}
--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/handler/IronGolemHandler.java
package pro.coreservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntitySpawnReason;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityTypes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.golem.IronGolem;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.npc.villager.Villager;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;

import java.util.List;

public class IronGolemHandler {
    public static final String CREATE_VILLAGER_TAG = "CreateVillager";
    private static final Component CREATE_VILLAGER_NAME = Component.literal(CREATE_VILLAGER_TAG);
    private static final int VILLAGER_CHECK_INTERVAL_TICKS = 20 * 10;
    private static final int GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS = 16;

    public static void register(MinecraftServer server) {
        // Initial setup
    }

    public static void tick(ServerLevel world) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.iron_golem_spawn_enabled) return;

        long gameTime = world.getGameTime();

        if (gameTime % VILLAGER_CHECK_INTERVAL_TICKS == 0) {
            updateCreateVillagers(world);
        }

        int intervalTicks = 20 * CoreServerMod.CONFIG.iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds;
        if (gameTime % intervalTicks == 0) {
            spawnGolemsForCreateVillagers(world);
        }
    }

    private static void updateCreateVillagers(ServerLevel world) {
        for (Villager villager : world.getEntities(EntityTypes.VILLAGER, villager -> true)) {
            boolean onIronBlock = isStandingOnIronBlock(world, villager);
            boolean hasTag = villager.entityTags().contains(CREATE_VILLAGER_TAG);

            if (onIronBlock) {
                if (!hasTag) {
                    villager.addTag(CREATE_VILLAGER_TAG);
                }
                if (!CREATE_VILLAGER_NAME.equals(villager.getCustomName())) {
                    villager.setCustomName(CREATE_VILLAGER_NAME);
                    villager.setCustomNameVisible(true);
                }
            } else if (hasTag) {
                villager.removeTag(CREATE_VILLAGER_TAG);
                if (CREATE_VILLAGER_NAME.equals(villager.getCustomName())) {
                    villager.setCustomName(null);
                    villager.setCustomNameVisible(false);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private static void spawnGolemsForCreateVillagers(ServerLevel world) {
        for (Villager villager : world.getEntities(EntityTypes.VILLAGER, villager -> true)) {
            if (!villager.entityTags().contains(CREATE_VILLAGER_TAG)) continue;
            if (!isStandingOnIronBlock(world, villager)) continue;
            if (hasReachedGolemLimit(world, villager.blockPosition())) continue;
            if (canSpawnGolem(world, villager.blockPosition())) {
                spawnGolem(world, villager.blockPosition());
            }
        }
    }

    private static boolean isStandingOnIronBlock(ServerLevel world, Villager villager) {
        BlockPos below = villager.blockPosition().below();
        return world.isLoaded(below) && world.getBlockState(below).is(Blocks.IRON_BLOCK);
    }

    private static boolean hasReachedGolemLimit(ServerLevel world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        int max = CoreServerMod.CONFIG.max_automatic_golems_per_area;
        if (max <= 0) return false;

        List<IronGolem> nearby = world.getEntities(
                EntityTypes.IRON_GOLEM,
                new AABB(villagerPos).inflate(GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS),
                golem -> true
        );
        return nearby.size() >= max;
    }

    private static boolean canSpawnGolem(ServerLevel world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        for (int x = -GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; x <= GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; x += 4) {
            for (int z = -GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; z <= GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; z += 4) {
                for (int y = -4; y <= 4; y++) {
                    BlockPos checkPos = villagerPos.offset(x, y, z);
                    if (isValidSpawnPosition(world, checkPos)) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static boolean isValidSpawnPosition(ServerLevel world, BlockPos pos) {
        BlockState state = world.getBlockState(pos);
        BlockState above = world.getBlockState(pos.above());
        BlockState below = world.getBlockState(pos.below());

        if (!state.isAir() || !above.isAir()) return false;
        if (!below.isFaceSturdy(world, pos.below(), Direction.UP)) return false;

        if (!state.getFluidState().isEmpty() || !above.getFluidState().isEmpty()) return false;

        AABB box = EntityTypes.IRON_GOLEM.getDimensions()
                .makeBoundingBox(Vec3.atCenterOf(pos));
        return world.noCollision(box);
    }

    private static void spawnGolem(ServerLevel world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        for (int x = -GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; x <= GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; x += 4) {
            for (int z = -GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; z <= GOLEM_SEARCH_RADIUS; z += 4) {
                for (int y = -4; y <= 4; y++) {
                    BlockPos spawnPos = villagerPos.offset(x, y, z);
                    if (isValidSpawnPosition(world, spawnPos)) {
                        IronGolem golem = EntityTypes.IRON_GOLEM.create(world, EntitySpawnReason.TRIGGERED);
                        if (golem != null) {
                            golem.snapTo(Vec3.atCenterOf(spawnPos), 0.0F, 0.0F);
                            golem.setPersistenceRequired();
                            world.addFreshEntity(golem);
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        // No cached state to clean up.
    }
}
--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/handler/SaplingHandler.java
package pro.coreservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.SaplingBlock;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;

import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class SaplingHandler {
    private static final Set<BlockPos> TRACKED_SAPLINGS = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();

    public static void register(MinecraftServer server) {
        // Could do initial scan here
    }

    public static void trackSapling(ServerLevel world, BlockPos pos) {
        if (world.getBlockState(pos).getBlock() instanceof SaplingBlock) {
            TRACKED_SAPLINGS.add(pos.immutable());
        }
    }

    public static void untrackSapling(BlockPos pos) {
        TRACKED_SAPLINGS.remove(pos);
    }

    public static void tick(ServerLevel world) {
        if (TRACKED_SAPLINGS.isEmpty()) return;

        int intervalTicks = 20 * CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sapling_growth_time_seconds;
        if (world.getGameTime() % intervalTicks != 0) return;

        for (BlockPos pos : new java.util.HashSet<>(TRACKED_SAPLINGS)) {
            if (!world.isLoaded(pos)) continue;

            if (!(world.getBlockState(pos).getBlock() instanceof SaplingBlock)) {
                TRACKED_SAPLINGS.remove(pos);
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        TRACKED_SAPLINGS.clear();
    }
}
--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/handler/SheepWoolHandler.java
package pro.coreservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.sheep.Sheep;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class SheepWoolHandler {
    private static final Map<Integer, Long> SHEARED_SHEEP = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public static void register(MinecraftServer server) {
        // Initial setup
    }

    public static void scheduleRegrowth(ServerLevel world, int sheepId) {
        SHEARED_SHEEP.put(sheepId, world.getGameTime());
    }

    public static void tick(ServerLevel world) {
        int intervalTicks = 20 * CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sheep_wool_regrowth_seconds;
        if (world.getGameTime() % intervalTicks != 0) return;

        long currentTime = world.getGameTime();

        for (Map.Entry<Integer, Long> entry : new java.util.HashMap<>(SHEARED_SHEEP).entrySet()) {
            int sheepId = entry.getKey();
            long shearTime = entry.getValue();

            if (currentTime - shearTime >= intervalTicks) {
                Entity entity = world.getEntity(sheepId);
                if (entity instanceof Sheep sheep) {
                    if (sheep.isSheared()) {
                        sheep.setSheared(false);
                        SHEARED_SHEEP.remove(sheepId);
                    }
                } else {
                    SHEARED_SHEEP.remove(sheepId);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        SHEARED_SHEEP.clear();
    }
}
--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/handler/SugarCaneHandler.java
package pro.coreservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;

import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class SugarCaneHandler {
    private static final Set<BlockPos> TRACKED_POSITIONS = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();

    public static void register(MinecraftServer server) {
        // Initial scan could be added here if needed
    }

    public static void trackPosition(ServerLevel world, BlockPos pos) {
        if (world.getBlockState(pos.below()).is(Blocks.SOUL_SAND)) {
            TRACKED_POSITIONS.add(pos.immutable());
        }
    }

    public static void untrackPosition(BlockPos pos) {
        TRACKED_POSITIONS.remove(pos);
    }

    public static void tick(ServerLevel world) {
        if (TRACKED_POSITIONS.isEmpty()) return;

        int intervalTicks = 20 * CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sugar_cane_growth_interval_seconds;
        if (world.getGameTime() % intervalTicks != 0) return;

        for (BlockPos pos : new HashSet<>(TRACKED_POSITIONS)) {
            if (!world.isLoaded(pos)) continue;

            if (!world.getBlockState(pos).is(Blocks.SUGAR_CANE)) {
                TRACKED_POSITIONS.remove(pos);
                continue;
            }

            if (!world.getBlockState(pos.below()).is(Blocks.SOUL_SAND)) {
                TRACKED_POSITIONS.remove(pos);
                continue;
            }

            int height = 1;
            while (height < 3 && world.getBlockState(pos.above(height)).is(Blocks.SUGAR_CANE)) {
                height++;
            }

            if (height < 3 && world.getBlockState(pos.above(height)).isAir()) {
                world.setBlockAndUpdate(pos.above(height), Blocks.SUGAR_CANE.defaultBlockState());
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        TRACKED_POSITIONS.clear();
    }
}
--- src/main/java/pro/coreservermod/handler/VillagerEmeraldHandler.java
package pro.coreservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityTypes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.npc.villager.Villager;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import pro.coreservermod.CoreServerMod;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class VillagerEmeraldHandler {
    private static final Map<Integer, Long> TRADE_COOLDOWNS = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private static final RandomSource RANDOM = RandomSource.create();

    public static void register(MinecraftServer server) {
        // Initial setup
    }

    public static void onTradeCompleted(ServerLevel world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        if (!CoreServerMod.CONFIG.villager_emerald_drop_enabled) return;

        List<Villager> villagers = world.getEntities(
                EntityTypes.VILLAGER,
                new AABB(villagerPos).inflate(2),
                villager -> villager.blockPosition().equals(villagerPos)
        );

        if (villagers.isEmpty()) return;
        Villager villager = villagers.get(0);

        int villagerId = villager.getId();
        long currentTime = world.getGameTime();

        Long lastTrade = TRADE_COOLDOWNS.get(villagerId);
        if (lastTrade != null && currentTime - lastTrade < 20) {
            return;
        }

        TRADE_COOLDOWNS.put(villagerId, currentTime);

        if (RANDOM.nextDouble() < CoreServerMod.CONFIG.villager_emerald_drop_chance) {
            ItemStack emerald = new ItemStack(Items.EMERALD, 1);
            villager.spawnAtLocation(world, emerald);
        }
    }

    public static void tick(ServerLevel world) {
        if (world.getGameTime() % 20 != 0) return;

        long currentTime = world.getGameTime();
        TRADE_COOLDOWNS.entrySet().removeIf(entry -> currentTime - entry.getValue() > 24000);
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        TRADE_COOLDOWNS.clear();
    }
}

.github/workflows/opencode.yml
name: Issue to OpenCode

on:
    issues:
        types: [opened]
    issue_comment:
        types: [created]

permissions: write-all

concurrency:
    group: issue-opencode-${{ github.event.issue.number }}
    cancel-in-progress: false

jobs:
    opencode:
        if: >-
            github.event_name == 'issues' ||
            (
                github.event_name == 'issue_comment' &&
                github.event.issue.pull_request == null &&
                github.event.comment.user.type != 'Bot'
            )

        runs-on: ubuntu-latest

        defaults:
            run:
                shell: bash
                working-directory: ${{ github.workspace }}

        steps:
            - name: Checkout repository
              uses: actions/checkout@v4
              with:
                  fetch-depth: 0
                  ref: ${{ github.event.repository.default_branch }}
                  token: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

            - name: Setup Node.js
              uses: actions/setup-node@v4
              with:
                  node-version: 22

            - name: Install OpenCode
              run: npm install -g opencode-ai@latest

            - name: Configure OpenCode
              run: |
                  set -euo pipefail

                  mkdir -p "$HOME/.config/opencode"

                  cat > "$HOME/.config/opencode/opencode.jsonc" <<'JSON'
                  {
                    "$schema": "https://opencode.ai/config.json",
                    "permission": "allow",
                    "model": "opencode/deepseek-v4.1-flash-free"
                  }
                  JSON

            - name: Prepare session directory
              run: mkdir -p .opencode-sessions

            - name: Import OpenCode session
              id: import_session
              env:
                  ISSUE_NUMBER: ${{ github.event.issue.number }}
              run: |
                  set -euo pipefail

                  session_file=".opencode-sessions/issue-${ISSUE_NUMBER}.json"

                  if [ -s "$session_file" ]; then
                      echo "Importing previous session..."

                      opencode import "$session_file"

                      echo "found=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
                  else
                      echo "No previous session found."
                      echo "found=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
                  fi

            - name: Prepare OpenCode query
              env:
                  EVENT_NAME: ${{ github.event_name }}
                  ISSUE_NUMBER: ${{ github.event.issue.number }}
                  ISSUE_TITLE: ${{ github.event.issue.title }}
                  ISSUE_BODY: ${{ github.event.issue.body }}
                  ISSUE_URL: ${{ github.event.issue.html_url }}
                  COMMENT_BODY: ${{ github.event.comment.body }}
                  COMMENT_AUTHOR: ${{ github.event.comment.user.login }}
              run: |
                  set -euo pipefail

                  python3 - <<'PY'
                  import os

                  event_name = os.environ["EVENT_NAME"]
                  issue_number = os.environ["ISSUE_NUMBER"]
                  issue_url = os.environ["ISSUE_URL"]

                  if event_name == "issue_comment":
                      query = f"""A new comment was added to GitHub issue #{issue_number}.

                  Issue URL:
                  {issue_url}

                  Comment author:
                  {os.environ.get('COMMENT_AUTHOR', '')}

                  Comment:
                  {os.environ.get('COMMENT_BODY') or ''}

                  You are a github agent that running on a GitHub action runner. You have complete access to this repo so do what you need to do for fux this problem.
                  After your task complete you should push you chnages to git repo or those fixes can be vanished.
                  Try your best for fixing the problem.
                  Do not ask questions; make reasonable decisions and proceed.
                  """
                  else:
                      query = f"""A new GitHub issue has been opened.

                  Issue number: {issue_number}

                  Issue URL:
                  {issue_url}

                  Title:
                  {os.environ.get('ISSUE_TITLE') or '(No title)'}

                  Description:
                  {os.environ.get('ISSUE_BODY') or '(No description)'}

                  Inspect the repository and implement the requested change.

                  You are a github agent that running on a GitHub action runner. You have complete access to this repo so do what you need to do for fux this problem.
                  After your task complete you should push you chnages to git repo or those fixes can be vanished.
                  Try your best for fixing the problem.
                  Do not ask questions; make reasonable decisions and proceed.
                  """

                  with open("issue-query.txt", "w", encoding="utf-8") as file:
                      file.write(query)
                  PY

            - name: Run OpenCode
              id: opencode
              env:
                  SESSION_FOUND: ${{ steps.import_session.outputs.found }}
              run: |
                  set -euo pipefail

                  prompt="$(cat issue-query.txt)"

                  if [ "$SESSION_FOUND" = "true" ]; then
                      echo "Continuing the latest OpenCode session..."

                      opencode run \
                          --model opencode/deepseek-v4.1-flash-free \
                          --continue \
                          --auto \
                          "$prompt" 2>&1 | tee opencode-output.txt
                  else
                      echo "Starting a new OpenCode session..."

                      opencode run \
                          --model opencode/deepseek-v4.1-flash-free \
                          --auto \
                          "$prompt" 2>&1 | tee opencode-output.txt
                  fi

            - name: Commit and push changes
              if: always()
              env:
                  ISSUE_NUMBER: ${{ github.event.issue.number }}
                  DEFAULT_BRANCH: ${{ github.event.repository.default_branch }}
              run: |
                  set -euo pipefail

                  git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
                  git config user.email \
                      "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"

                  git add -A

                  if git diff --cached --quiet; then
                      echo "No changes to commit."
                      exit 0
                  fi

                  git commit -m "Work on issue #${ISSUE_NUMBER}"

                  git push origin "HEAD:${DEFAULT_BRANCH}"

            - name: Comment with OpenCode result
              if: always()
              uses: actions/github-script@v7
              env:
                  ISSUE_NUMBER: ${{ github.event.issue.number }}
                  RUN_OUTCOME: ${{ steps.opencode.outcome }}
              with:
                  script: |
                      const fs = require('fs');

                      const issueNumber = Number(process.env.ISSUE_NUMBER);
                      const outcome = process.env.RUN_OUTCOME || 'unknown';

                      let output = '';

                      if (fs.existsSync('opencode-output.txt')) {
                          output = fs.readFileSync(
                              'opencode-output.txt',
                              'utf8'
                          ).trim();
                      }

                      if (output.length > 30000) {
                          output = output.slice(-30000);
                      }

                      const body = [
                          `### OpenCode run: ${outcome}`,
                          '',
                          output
                              ? '```text\n' +
                                output.replace(/```/g, "'''") +
                                '\n```'
                              : 'OpenCode did not produce captured output.',
                          '',
                          `Workflow run: ${context.serverUrl}/${context.repo.owner}/${context.repo.repo}/actions/runs/${context.runId}`
                      ].join('\n');

                      await github.rest.issues.createComment({
                          owner: context.repo.owner,
                          repo: context.repo.repo,
                          issue_number: issueNumber,
                          body
                      });

package pro.codeservermod.mixin;

import net.minecraft.block.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.block.CropBlock;
import net.minecraft.server.world.ServerWorld;
import net.minecraft.util.math.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.util.math.random.Random;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod;

@Mixin(CropBlock.class)
public class CropBlockMixin {
    @Inject(method = "randomTick", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
    private void randomTick(BlockState state, ServerWorld world, BlockPos pos, Random random, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (!(CoreServerMod.CONFIG.crop_growth_time_seconds > 0)) return;
        
        CropBlock crop = (CropBlock) (Object) this;
        int maxAge = crop.getMaxAge();
        int currentAge = crop.getAge(state);
        
        if (currentAge < maxAge) {
            if (random.nextInt(20 * CoreServerMod.CONFIG.crop_growth_time_seconds) == 0) {
                world.setBlockState(pos, crop.withAge(currentAge + 1));
            }
            ci.cancel();
        }
    }
}package pro.codeservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.block.SaplingBlock;
import net.minecraft.server.world.ServerWorld;
import net.minecraft.util.math.BlockPos;

import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class SaplingHandler {
    private static final Set<BlockPos> TRACKED_SAPLINGS = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
    private static int tickCounter = 0;

    public static void register(net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer server) {
        // Could do initial scan here
    }

    public static void trackSapling(ServerWorld world, BlockPos pos) {
        if (world.getBlockState(pos).getBlock() instanceof SaplingBlock) {
            TRACKED_SAPLINGS.add(pos.toImmutable());
        }
    }

    public static void untrackSapling(BlockPos pos) {
        TRACKED_SAPLINGS.remove(pos);
    }

    public static void tick(ServerWorld world) {
        tickCounter++;
        int intervalTicks = 20 * pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.sapling_growth_time_seconds;
        
        if (tickCounter % intervalTicks != 0) return;
        
        for (BlockPos pos : new java.util.HashSet<>(TRACKED_SAPLINGS)) {
            if (!world.isChunkLoaded(pos.getX() >> 4, pos.getZ() >> 4)) continue;
            
            if (!(world.getBlockState(pos).getBlock() instanceof SaplingBlock)) {
                TRACKED_SAPLINGS.remove(pos);
                continue;
            }
            
            // Let the vanilla generator handle it with our mixin accelerating the chance
            // The mixin will handle the actual generation
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        TRACKED_SAPLINGS.clear();
    }
}

package pro.codeservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.entity.passive.ChickenEntity;
import net.minecraft.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.server.world.ServerWorld;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class ChickenEggHandler {
    private static final Map<Integer, Long> CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private static int tickCounter = 0;

    public static void register(net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer server) {
        // Setup
    }

    public static void scheduleEggLay(ServerWorld world, int chickenId) {
        long currentTime = world.getTime();
        Long lastLay = CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS.get(chickenId);
        
        if (lastLay == null) {
            CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS.put(chickenId, currentTime);
        }
    }

    public static void tick(ServerWorld world) {
        tickCounter++;
        int intervalTicks = 20 * pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.chicken_egg_interval_seconds;
        
        if (tickCounter % intervalTicks != 0) return;
        
        long currentTime = world.getTime();
        
        for (Map.Entry<Integer, Long> entry : new java.util.HashMap<>(CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS).entrySet()) {
            int chickenId = entry.getKey();
            long lastLay = entry.getValue();
            
            if (currentTime - lastLay >= intervalTicks) {
                net.minecraft.entity.Entity entity = world.getEntityById(chickenId);
                if (entity instanceof ChickenEntity chicken && !chicken.isBaby()) {
                    ItemStack egg = new ItemStack(Items.EGG, 1);
                    chicken.dropStack(world, egg);
                    CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS.put(chickenId, currentTime);
                } else if (entity == null || entity instanceof ChickenEntity chicken && chicken.isBaby()) {
                    CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS.remove(chickenId);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        CHICKEN_EGG_TIMERS.clear();
    }
}
--- old iron ---
package pro.codeservermod.handler;

import net.minecraft.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.entity.passive.IronGolemEntity;
import net.minecraft.entity.passive.VillagerEntity;
import net.minecraft.server.world.ServerWorld;
import net.minecraft.util.math.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.util.math.Box;
import net.minecraft.util.math.Vec3d;
import net.minecraft.entity.SpawnReason;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedQueue;

public class IronGolemHandler {
    private static final Map<BlockPos, Long> VILLAGER_IRON_BLOCK_TIMES = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private static int tickCounter = 0;

    public static void register(net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer server) {
        // Initial setup
    }

    public static void scheduleGolemCheck(ServerWorld world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        // Check if the villager is on an iron block
        if (world.getBlockState(villagerPos.down()).isOf(Blocks.IRON_BLOCK)) {
            VILLAGER_IRON_BLOCK_TIMES.put(villagerPos.toImmutable(), world.getTime());
        }
    }

    public static void tick(ServerWorld world) {
        tickCounter++;
        int intervalTicks = 20 * pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.iron_golem_spawn_interval_seconds;
        
        if (tickCounter % intervalTicks != 0) return;
        
        if (!pro.codeservermod.CoreServerMod.CONFIG.iron_golem_spawn_enabled) return;

        // Clean up entries for villagers no longer on iron blocks
        for (Map.Entry<BlockPos, Long> entry : new HashMap<>(VILLAGER_IRON_BLOCK_TIMES).entrySet()) {
            BlockPos villagerPos = entry.getKey();
            
            if (!world.isChunkLoaded(villagerPos.getX() >> 4, villagerPos.getZ() >> 4)) continue;
            
            // Check if villager is still on iron block
            if (!world.getBlockState(villagerPos.down()).isOf(Blocks.IRON_BLOCK)) {
                VILLAGER_IRON_BLOCK_TIMES.remove(villagerPos);
                continue;
            }
            
            // Check for valid spawn position
            if (canSpawnGolem(world, villagerPos)) {
                spawnGolem(world, villagerPos);
            }
        }
    }

    private static boolean canSpawnGolem(ServerWorld world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        // Check if there's a valid spawn position near the villager
        for (int x = -16; x <= 16; x += 4) {
            for (int z = -16; z <= 16; z += 4) {
                for (int y = -4; y <= 4; y++) {
                    BlockPos checkPos = villagerPos.add(x, y, z);
                    if (isValidSpawnPosition(world, checkPos)) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static boolean isValidSpawnPosition(ServerWorld world, BlockPos pos) {
        // Check if position is air and block below is solid
        if (!world.getBlockState(pos).isAir()) return false;
        if (!world.getBlockState(pos.up()).isAir()) return false;
        if (!world.getBlockState(pos.down()).isSideSolidFullSquare(world, pos.down(), net.minecraft.util.math.Direction.UP)) return false;
        
        // Check not in liquid
        if (world.getBlockState(pos).getFluidState().isStill()) return false;
        if (world.getBlockState(pos.up()).getFluidState().isStill()) return false;
        
        // Check collision
        Box box = EntityType.IRON_GOLEM.getDimensions().getBoxAt(Vec3d.ofCenter(pos));
        Entity testEntity = EntityType.IRON_GOLEM.create(world, SpawnReason.TRIGGERED);
        if (testEntity == null || !world.isSpaceEmpty(testEntity, box)) return false;
        
        return true;
    }

    private static void spawnGolem(ServerWorld world, BlockPos villagerPos) {
        for (int x = -16; x <= 16; x += 4) {
            for (int z = -16; z <= 16; z += 4) {
                for (int y = -4; y <= 4; y++) {
                    BlockPos spawnPos = villagerPos.add(x, y, z);
                    if (isValidSpawnPosition(world, spawnPos)) {
                        IronGolemEntity golem = EntityType.IRON_GOLEM.create(world, SpawnReason.TRIGGERED);
                        if (golem != null) {
                            golem.refreshPositionAndAngles(Vec3d.ofCenter(spawnPos), 0, 0);
                            golem.setPersistent();
                            world.spawnEntity(golem);
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public static void cleanup() {
        VILLAGER_IRON_BLOCK_TIMES.clear();
    }
}

[0m$ [0mcat stop-test-server.sh 2>/dev/null; printf '\n--- resources config ---\n'; cat src/main/resources/config/coreservermod.json; printf '\n--- dist jar manifest ---\n'; unzip -p dist/CoreServerMod-1.0.0.jar fabric.mod.json | head -40
#!/bin/bash
# CoreServerMod Test Server Stop Script
# Safely stops the test server

set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
SERVER_DIR="$SCRIPT_DIR/test-server"
PID_FILE="$SERVER_DIR/server.pid"

echo "=== CoreServerMod Test Server Stop ==="

# Try to find server process
if [ -f "$PID_FILE" ]; then
    PID=$(cat "$PID_FILE" 2>/dev/null)
    if [ -n "$PID" ] && kill -0 "$PID" 2>/dev/null; then
        echo "Stopping server (PID: $PID)..."
        kill -TERM "$PID"
        
        # Wait for graceful shutdown
        for i in {1..30}; do
            if ! kill -0 "$PID" 2>/dev/null; then
                echo "Server stopped gracefully."
                rm -f "$PID_FILE"
                exit 0
            fi
            sleep 1
        done
        
        # Force kill if still running
        echo "Server did not stop gracefully, forcing..."
        kill -KILL "$PID" 2>/dev/null
        sleep 2
        rm -f "$PID_FILE"
        echo "Server forcefully stopped."
        exit 0
    fi
fi

# Fallback: find by port
PID=$(lsof -ti:25565 2>/dev/null | head -1)
if [ -n "$PID" ]; then
    echo "Found server on port 25565 (PID: $PID)"
    kill -TERM "$PID"
    sleep 5
    if kill -0 "$PID" 2>/dev/null; then
        kill -KILL "$PID"
    fi
    echo "Server stopped."
    exit 0
fi

echo "No running server found."
exit 0

#!/bin/bash
# CoreServerMod Build Script
# Builds the Fabric mod JAR

set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"

echo "=== CoreServerMod Build Script ==="
echo "Working directory: $SCRIPT_DIR"

# Check for Gradle wrapper
if [ ! -f "./gradlew" ]; then
    echo "Generating Gradle wrapper..."
    gradle wrapper --gradle-version 9.8.1
fi

# Make gradlew executable
chmod +x ./gradlew

# Build the mod
echo "Building CoreServerMod..."
./gradlew clean build -x test

# Copy the release JAR to dist
mkdir -p dist
cp build/libs/CoreServerMod-*.jar dist/

# Check if build was successful
JAR_PATH=$(find build/libs -name "CoreServerMod-*.jar" | head -1)
if [ -n "$JAR_PATH" ] && [ -f "$JAR_PATH" ]; then
    echo "Build successful! JAR: $JAR_PATH"
    
    # Copy to distribution and test server mods directories
    mkdir -p dist test-server/mods
    cp "$JAR_PATH" dist/
    cp "$JAR_PATH" test-server/mods/
    echo "Copied JAR to dist/ and test-server/mods/"
else
    echo "Build failed! JAR not found."
    exit 1
fi

echo "=== Build Complete ==="
--- start head ---
#!/bin/bash
# CoreServerMod Test Server Start Script
# Launches a dedicated Minecraft 26.2 Fabric server for testing

set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"

SERVER_DIR="$SCRIPT_DIR/test-server"
MODS_DIR="$SERVER_DIR/mods"
CONFIG_DIR="$SERVER_DIR/config"
WORLD_DIR="$SERVER_DIR/world"
LOGS_DIR="$SERVER_DIR/logs"

# Server configuration
MINECRAFT_VERSION="26.2"
FABRIC_LOADER_VERSION="0.20.1"
FABRIC_API_VERSION="0.161.0+26.2"
JAVA_VERSION="25"
SERVER_JAR="fabric-server-mc${MINECRAFT_VERSION}-loader${FABRIC_LOADER_VERSION}-launcher1.0.1.jar"
SERVER_URL="https://meta.fabricmc.net/v2/versions/loader/${MINECRAFT_VERSION}/${FABRIC_LOADER_VERSION}/1.0.1/server/jar"
FABRIC_API_URL="https://maven.fabricmc.net/net/fabricmc/fabric-api/fabric-api/${FABRIC_API_VERSION}/fabric-api-${FABRIC_API_VERSION}.jar"

# Memory settings
HEAP_MIN="2G"
HEAP_MAX="4G"

echo "=== CoreServerMod Test Server ==="
echo "Server directory: $SERVER_DIR"
echo "Minecraft version: $MINECRAFT_VERSION"
echo "Fabric Loader: $FABRIC_LOADER_VERSION"

# Create directories
mkdir -p "$MODS_DIR" "$CONFIG_DIR" "$WORLD_DIR" "$LOGS_DIR"

# Check for Java
if ! command -v java &> /dev/null; then
    echo "ERROR: Java not found. Please install Java $JAVA_VERSION or later."
    exit 1
fi

JAVA_VER=$(java -version 2>&1 | head -1 | cut -d'"' -f2 | cut -d'.' -f1)
if [ "$JAVA_VER" -lt "$JAVA_VERSION" ]; then
    echo "WARNING: Java version $JAVA_VER detected, recommended is $JAVA_VERSION+"
fi

# Download Fabric server JAR if not present
if [ ! -f "$SERVER_DIR/$SERVER_JAR" ]; then
    echo "Downloading Fabric server JAR..."
    cd "$SERVER_DIR"
    curl -L -o "$SERVER_JAR" "$SERVER_URL"
    echo "Download complete."
fi

# Download Fabric API if not present
FABRIC_API_JAR="fabric-api-${FABRIC_API_VERSION}.jar"
if [ ! -f "$MODS_DIR/$FABRIC_API_JAR" ]; then
    echo "Downloading Fabric API..."
    curl -L -o "$MODS_DIR/$FABRIC_API_JAR" "$FABRIC_API_URL"
    echo "Download complete."
fi

# Build mod if not present
MOD_JAR=$(find "$MODS_DIR" -name "CoreServerMod-*.jar" | head -1)
if [ -z "$MOD_JAR" ] || [ ! -f "$MOD_JAR" ]; then
    echo "CoreServerMod JAR not found. Building..."
    cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"
    ./build.sh
    MOD_JAR=$(find "$MODS_DIR" -name "CoreServerMod-*.jar" | head -1)
fi

# Check EULA
EULA_FILE="$SERVER_DIR/eula.txt"
if [ ! -f "$EULA_FILE" ]; then
    echo ""
    echo "=========================================="
    echo "MINECRAFT EULA AGREEMENT REQUIRED"
    echo "=========================================="
    echo "By starting this server, you agree to the Minecraft EULA:"
[0m
